# Package net.thevpc.nuts.concurrent

<a id="pkg-net-thevpc-nuts-concurrent"></a>

Resilience, coordination, and execution utilities.



The package defines lock, retry, rate-limit, circuit-breaker, bulkhead,
cache/once-value, saga, timeout, scoped-value, and work-balancing contracts.
Models, factories, stores, and strategies separate policy from persistence and
allow the Nuts runtime or an integration to provide an implementation.

<a id="cls-net-thevpc-nuts-concurrent-NBulkheadCall"></a>

## NBulkheadCall

```java
public interface NBulkheadCall<T> extends NCallable<T>, NDescribable, AutoCloseable
```

Represents a callable task with bounded concurrency control.



An `NBulkheadCall` wraps a computation and ensures that at most N
instances of it execute concurrently. When the limit is reached, callers can
choose to wait, fail immediately, or provide fallback behavior.







Unlike [NRateLimitValue](#cls-NRateLimitValue), which controls the rate of requests over time,
this interface controls the number of simultaneously active executions. This is
useful for protecting resources that have hard concurrency limits (database
connections, API quotas, thread pools).







Example usage:
```java
NBulkheadCall<String> limiter = NBulkheadCall.of(() -> {
    return expensiveDatabaseQuery();)
.setMaxConcurrent(5);

// Try to execute, fail fast if limit reached
NOptional<String> result = limiter.tryCall();
if (result.isEmpty()) {
    // Handle rejection
}

// Or wait up to 1 second for a slot
String result = limiter.call(NDuration.ofSeconds(1));
}</String>
```



**Since:** 0.8.8

| :--- | :--- | :--- |

| `static NBulkheadCall&lt;T&gt;` | [of(String, NCallable&lt;T&gt;)](#m-NBulkheadCall-of) | Creates a new concurrency limiter with a specific identifier. |
| `NBulkheadCall&lt;T&gt;` | [permitExpiry(NDuration)](#m-NBulkheadCall-permitExpiry) | Sets permit expiry time for crash recovery. |
| `NBulkheadCall&lt;T&gt;` | [maxConcurrent(int)](#m-NBulkheadCall-maxConcurrent) | Sets the maximum number of concurrent executions allowed. |
| `NOptional&lt;T&gt;` | [tryCall()](#m-NBulkheadCall-tryCall) | Attempts to execute the callable immediately without waiting. |
| `T` | [tryCallOrElse(NCallable&lt;T&gt;)](#m-NBulkheadCall-tryCallOrElse) | Attempts to execute the callable, providing a fallback if rejected. |

<a id="m-NBulkheadCall-tryCall"></a>

#### tryCall

```java
NOptional<T> tryCall()
```

Attempts to execute the callable immediately without waiting.



If the concurrency limit has been reached, returns an empty [NOptional](#cls-NOptional)
immediately without blocking or executing the callable.

**Returns:**

optional containing the result if execution succeeded, empty if rejected



---

<a id="m-NBulkheadCall-tryCallOrElse"></a>

#### tryCallOrElse

```java
T tryCallOrElse(NCallable<T> fallback)
```

Attempts to execute the callable, providing a fallback if rejected.



If the concurrency limit has been reached, executes the fallback callable
instead of the main callable.

**Parameters:**

- `fallback` - callable to execute if rejected

**Returns:**

the result of either the main or fallback callable



---

<a id="m-NBulkheadCall-callBlocking"></a>

#### callBlocking

```java
T callBlocking() throws NInterruptedException
```

Executes the callable, waiting indefinitely for a slot if necessary.



This method blocks until the callable can be executed within the
concurrency limit. The thread may be interrupted while waiting.

**Returns:**

the result of the callable

**Throws:**

- `NInterruptedException` - if interrupted while waiting



---

<a id="m-NBulkheadCall-callBlocking"></a>

#### callBlocking

```java
T callBlocking(NDuration timeout) throws NInterruptedException
```

Executes the callable, waiting up to the specified timeout for a slot.



If a slot becomes available within the timeout, executes the callable.
Otherwise, throws [NConcurrencyLimitException](#cls-NConcurrencyLimitException).

**Parameters:**

- `timeout` - maximum time to wait

**Returns:**

the result of the callable

**Throws:**

- `NConcurrencyLimitException` - if timeout expires
- `NInterruptedException` - if interrupted while waiting



---

<a id="m-NBulkheadCall-callOrElse"></a>

#### callOrElse

```java
T callOrElse(NDuration timeout, NCallable<T> fallback) throws NInterruptedException
```

Executes the callable with timeout, providing a fallback on rejection or timeout.

**Parameters:**

- `timeout` - maximum time to wait
- `fallback` - callable to execute if timeout expires

**Returns:**

the result of either the main or fallback callable

**Throws:**

- `NInterruptedException` - if interrupted while waiting

<a id="cls-net-thevpc-nuts-concurrent-NBulkheadCallBackend"></a>

## NBulkheadCallBackend

```java
public interface NBulkheadCallBackend
```

Backend interface for implementing a bulkhead pattern for controlling concurrent access
to resources or services.



A bulkhead limits the number of concurrent executions identified by a `bulkheadId`.
This interface provides methods to acquire and release permits, retrieve metrics,
and clean up expired permits.



Typical usage:
```java
NBulkheadCallBackend backend = ...;
NOptional<NBulkheadCallBackend.NBulkheadPermit> permit = backend.tryAcquire("serviceA", 10);
if(permit.isPresent()){
    try {
        // execute protected code finally {
        backend.release(permit.get());
    }
}
}
```





**Since:** 0.8.8

### Method Summary

| Modifier and Type | Method | Description |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |

<a id="cls-net-thevpc-nuts-concurrent-NBulkheadCallFactory"></a>

## NBulkheadCallFactory

```java
public interface NBulkheadCallFactory
```

Factory interface for creating and configuring [NBulkheadCall](#cls-NBulkheadCall) instances.



A bulkhead call wraps a [NCallable](#cls-NCallable) with concurrency control, ensuring that
only a limited number of concurrent calls (per bulkhead ID) are allowed to execute.
This is part of the bulkhead pattern for fault isolation and system resilience.



Instances can be obtained using the static factory methods [#of()](#m-of) or [#of(NBulkheadCallStore)](#m-of).
Implementations typically rely on [NBulkheadCallBackend](#cls-NBulkheadCallBackend) to enforce concurrency limits
and on [NBulkheadCallStore](#cls-NBulkheadCallStore) to persist related state or metrics.



Example usage:
```java
NBulkheadCallFactory factory = NBulkheadCallFactory.of();

NBulkheadCall<String> call = factory.bulkheadCall("userService", () -> {
    return userService.fetchUserData(););

String result = call.execute();
}
```







**Since:** 0.8.8

### Method Summary

| Modifier and Type | Method | Description |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `static NBulkheadCallFactory` | [of()](#m-NBulkheadCallFactory-of) | returns global NBulkheadCallFactory instance NConcurrent configuration. |
| `static void` | [configure(NBulkheadCallFactory)](#m-NBulkheadCallFactory-configure) | set global NBulkheadCallFactory instance |
| `static NBulkheadCallFactory` | [ofDefault()](#m-NBulkheadCallFactory-ofDefault) |  |
| `static NBulkheadCallFactory` | [ofMem()](#m-NBulkheadCallFactory-ofMem) |  |
| `static NBulkheadCallFactory` | [of(NBulkheadCallStore)](#m-NBulkheadCallFactory-of) | Creates a NBulkheadCallFactory bound to a specific NBulkheadCallStore. |
| `NBulkheadCall&lt;T&gt;` | [bulkheadCall(NCallable&lt;T&gt;)](#m-NBulkheadCallFactory-bulkheadCall) | Wraps a callable with bulkhead protection, automatically assigning an internal bulkhead ID. |
| `NBulkheadCall&lt;T&gt;` | [bulkheadCall(String, NCallable&lt;T&gt;)](#m-NBulkheadCallFactory-bulkheadCall) | Wraps a callable with bulkhead protection, using a specific bulkhead identifier. |
| `NBulkheadCallFactory` | [withStore(NBulkheadCallStore)](#m-NBulkheadCallFactory-withStore) | Returns a new factory instance bound to the specified NBulkheadCallStore. |
| `NBulkheadCallFactory` | [withBackend(NBulkheadCallBackend)](#m-NBulkheadCallFactory-withBackend) | Returns a new factory instance using the specified NBulkheadCallBackend. |
| `NBulkheadCallStore` | [store()](#m-NBulkheadCallFactory-store) | Returns the currently configured NBulkheadCallStore. |
| `NBulkheadCallBackend` | [backend()](#m-NBulkheadCallFactory-backend) | Returns the currently configured NBulkheadCallBackend. |

<a id="cls-net-thevpc-nuts-concurrent-NBulkheadCallModel"></a>

## NBulkheadCallModel

```java
public class NBulkheadCallModel implements Cloneable, NCopiable
```

Represents the configuration and runtime metadata of a bulkhead-protected call.
A bulkhead call model defines concurrency limits and permit expiry rules,
allowing the system to restrict the number of concurrent invocations of a given task.

```java
public NBulkheadMetrics(String bulkheadId, int maxConcurrent, int activeCalls, int availableSlots, long totalAcquired, long totalReleased, long totalRejected, long maxWaitTimeMillis, long maxHoldTimeMillis)
```

Creates a new metrics snapshot for a bulkhead.

**Parameters:**

- `bulkheadId` - unique identifier of the bulkhead
- `maxConcurrent` - configured maximum number of concurrent calls
- `activeCalls` - number of currently active calls
- `availableSlots` - number of free slots available for new calls
- `totalAcquired` - cumulative number of successfully acquired permits
- `totalReleased` - cumulative number of released permits
- `totalRejected` - cumulative number of rejected acquisitions
- `maxWaitTimeMillis` - maximum observed wait time in milliseconds
- `maxHoldTimeMillis` - maximum observed permit hold time in milliseconds

<a id="cls-net-thevpc-nuts-concurrent-NCachedValue"></a>

## NCachedValue

```java
public interface NCachedValue<T> extends Supplier<T>, NDescribable
```

Represents a lazily computed and optionally cached value that can automatically
expire, retry loading, and retain its last valid result on failure.



An `NCachedValue<T>` wraps a `Supplier` of a value, caching its result
for a configurable duration (expiry). Once expired, the value may be recomputed
the next time it is requested. The cache also supports retry behavior to
control how often it reattempts loading after a failure.



Implementations are typically thread-safe and designed for concurrent access.





**Since:** 0.8.6

### Method Summary

| Modifier and Type | Method | Description |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `static NCachedValue&lt;T&gt;` | [of(Supplier&lt;T&gt;)](#m-NCachedValue-of) | Creates a new cached value that uses the given supplier. |
| `NCachedValue&lt;T&gt;` | [expiry(NDuration)](#m-NCachedValue-expiry) | Sets the expiry duration for the cached value. |
| `NCachedValue&lt;T&gt;` | [expiryMillis(long)](#m-NCachedValue-expiryMillis) | Sets the expiry duration in milliseconds. |
| `NCachedValue&lt;T&gt;` | [maxRetries(int)](#m-NCachedValue-maxRetries) | Sets the maximum number of retry attempts after a failed computation. |
| `NCachedValue&lt;T&gt;` | [retryPeriod(NDuration)](#m-NCachedValue-retryPeriod) | Sets the minimum period between retry attempts after a failed computation. |
| `NCachedValue&lt;T&gt;` | [retry(int, NDuration)](#m-NCachedValue-retry) | Sets both retry parameters at once: the maximum retry count and the retry period. |
| `NCachedValue&lt;T&gt;` | [retainLastOnFailure(boolean)](#m-NCachedValue-retainLastOnFailure) | Configures whether to retain the last successfully computed value when a subsequent computation fails. |
| `NCachedValue&lt;T&gt;` | [invalidate()](#m-NCachedValue-invalidate) | Invalidates the cached value immediately. |
| `boolean` | [isValid()](#m-NCachedValue-isValid) | Returns whether the current cached value is valid (not expired or invalidated). |
| `boolean` | [isError()](#m-NCachedValue-isError) | Returns whether the last computation resulted in an error. |
| `boolean` | [isEvaluated()](#m-NCachedValue-isEvaluated) | Returns whether the value has been computed at least once. |
| `boolean` | [isExpired()](#m-NCachedValue-isExpired) | Returns whether the cached value has expired and should be recomputed on the next access. |
| `T` | [get()](#m-NCachedValue-get) | Returns the current cached value, computing or recomputing it if necessary. |
| `NCachedValue&lt;T&gt;` | [update(Supplier&lt;T&gt;)](#m-NCachedValue-update) | Immediately computes a new value using the provided supplier and stores it in the cache. |
| `NCachedValue&lt;T&gt;` | [update()](#m-NCachedValue-update) | Forces an immediate recomputation of the cached value using the internal supplier. |
| `NCachedValue&lt;T&gt;` | [value(T)](#m-NCachedValue-value) | Directly sets the cached value and marks it as valid. |

| `boolean` | [setValueIfInvalid(T)](#m-NCachedValue-setValueIfInvalid) | Sets a provided value only if the current one is invalid or expired. |

```java
@NGetter
```

A retry period of zero means that a new attempt will be made immediately
on the next access. A non-zero period delays retries to avoid hammering
a failing source.

<a id="m-NCachedValue-update"></a>

#### update

```java
NCachedValue<T> update(Supplier<T> supplier)
```

Immediately computes a new value using the provided supplier and stores it in the cache.



If a non-null `supplier` is provided, it is used once to compute
a new value, without replacing the instance’s configured supplier.
If `supplier` is `null`, the internal supplier is used instead,
behaving exactly like [#update()](#m-update).



The newly computed value replaces the current cached value, marks the cache
as valid (clears any previous error state), and resets the expiry timer.

**Parameters:**

- `supplier` - optional supplier used for this computation; if `null`,
                the internal supplier is used

**Returns:**

this instance

**Throws:**

- `RuntimeException` - if the computation fails and the cache is not configured
                         to retain the last valid value



---

<a id="m-NCachedValue-update"></a>

#### update

```java
NCachedValue<T> update()
```

Forces an immediate recomputation of the cached value using the internal supplier.



This method is equivalent to calling [#update(Supplier)](#m-update) with a `null`
argument. The internal supplier is invoked to compute a new value, which replaces
the current cached value. Upon success, the cache is marked as valid, any previous
error state is cleared, and the expiry timer is reset.

**Returns:**

this instance

**Throws:**

- `RuntimeException` - if the computation fails and the cache is not configured
                         to retain the last valid value



---

```java
@NSetter

```

**Mutator:** Property setter.

Directly sets the cached value and marks it as valid.

**Parameters:**

- `value` - the new cached value

**Returns:**

this instance



---

<a id="m-NCachedValue-computeAndSetIfInvalid"></a>

#### computeAndSetIfInvalid

```java
boolean computeAndSetIfInvalid(Supplier<T> supplier)
```

Computes and updates the cached value if it is currently invalid.



If the cached value is considered invalid — meaning it has never been
evaluated, has expired, or is in an error state — this method will
compute a new value using the provided `supplier`. The computed value
will then replace the old one in the cache and be marked as valid.



If the provided `supplier` is `null`, the internal supplier
(the one originally passed to the cached value at construction) is used instead.
This allows recomputation using the cache's default logic without passing
a supplier explicitly.



If the cached value is already valid, this method does nothing and returns `false`.



This method is synchronized internally to ensure atomic evaluation and update.

**Parameters:**

- `supplier` - the value supplier to use for recomputation, or `null`
                to fallback to the internal supplier

**Returns:**

`true` if a new value was computed and set, `false` if the
cached value was already valid

**Throws:**

- `RuntimeException` - if the computation fails and no previous valid value
                         can be retained



---

<a id="m-NCachedValue-setValueIfInvalid"></a>

#### setValueIfInvalid

```java
boolean setValueIfInvalid(T value)
```

Sets a provided value only if the current one is invalid or expired.

**Parameters:**

- `value` - the value to set

**Returns:**

`true` if the provided value was set



---

<a id="cls-net-thevpc-nuts-concurrent-NCachedValueFactory"></a>

## NCachedValueFactory

```java
public interface NCachedValueFactory
```

Factory interface for creating [NCachedValue](#cls-NCachedValue) instances.



A `NCachedValueFactory` is responsible for producing cached value wrappers
that lazily evaluate and store a value produced by a `Supplier`.
Implementations may support various caching strategies (e.g., memory-based,
time-based, or thread-local caching).







Example usage:
```java
NCachedValueFactory factory = ...;
NCachedValue<String> cached = factory.of("config-value", () -> loadConfig());

// The supplier is called only once until cache invalidation
String v1 = cached.get();
String v2 = cached.get(); // returns cached result
```




**Since:** 0.8.6

### Method Summary

| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `static NCachedValueFactory` | [of()](#m-NCachedValueFactory-of) | Creates a new NCachedValueFactory using the default store. |
| `static NCachedValueFactory` | [of(NCachedValueStore)](#m-NCachedValueFactory-of) | Creates a new NCachedValueFactory with a custom NCachedValueStore. |
| `static NCachedValueFactory` | [ofMem()](#m-NCachedValueFactory-ofMem) |  |
| `static NCachedValueFactory` | [ofDefault()](#m-NCachedValueFactory-ofDefault) |  |
| `static void` | [configure(NCachedValueFactory)](#m-NCachedValueFactory-configure) | Sets the cached the default value factory used by this component. |
| `NCachedValueFactory` | [withStore(NCachedValueStore)](#m-NCachedValueFactory-withStore) | Sets the backing NCachedValueStore for this factory. |
| `NCachedValueStore` | [store()](#m-NCachedValueFactory-store) | Returns the backing store used by this factory. |
| `NCachedValue&lt;T&gt;` | [of(String, Supplier&lt;T&gt;)](#m-NCachedValueFactory-of) | Creates a named cached value for the given supplier. |
| `NCachedValue&lt;T&gt;` | [of(Supplier&lt;T&gt;)](#m-NCachedValueFactory-of) | Creates an unnamed cached value for the given supplier. |

<a id="cls-net-thevpc-nuts-concurrent-NCachedValueModel"></a>

## NCachedValueModel

```java
public class NCachedValueModel implements Cloneable, NCopiable
```

Internal data model representing the persisted state of a cached value.



This class is not intended for direct use by application developers.
It is used internally by [NCachedValueFactory](#cls-NCachedValueFactory) and [NCachedValueStore](#cls-NCachedValueStore)
implementations to store and exchange metadata associated with cached values.







The model holds all the information required to manage the lifecycle
of a cached entry, including:





- the current and last valid values
- timestamps and retry counters
- expiry and retry configuration
- failure tracking and invalidation flags






Implementations of [NCachedValueStore](#cls-NCachedValueStore) are responsible for
persisting or reconstructing this model when cache entries are loaded or saved.




**Since:** 0.8.6

### Fields

| Modifier and Type | Field | Description |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `private String` | `id` | Unique identifier for the cached entry. |
| `private Object` | `value` | The most recently computed value (may be null). |
| `private Throwable` | `error` | The last thrown exception during value computation, if any. |
| `private boolean` | `invalidated` | Indicates whether the cache entry has been explicitly invalidated. |
| `private Boolean` | `errorState` | Whether the cache is in an error state (e.g., repeated failures). |
| `private Object` | `lastValidValue` | The last known valid value, used if retainLastOnFailure is enabled. |
| `private long` | `lastEvalTimestamp` | Timestamp of the last successful or attempted evaluation, in milliseconds. |
| `private int` | `failedAttempts` | Number of consecutive failed attempts since last success. |
| `private NDuration` | `expiry` | Maximum duration before the cached value expires. |
| `private NDuration` | `retryPeriod` | Minimum wait period before retrying after a failure. |
| `private int` | `maxRetries` | Maximum number of retries allowed after failure before marking error state. |
| `private boolean` | `retainLastOnFailure` | Whether to retain the last valid value if computation fails. |

<a id="cls-net-thevpc-nuts-concurrent-NCachedValueStore"></a>

## NCachedValueStore

```java
public interface NCachedValueStore
```

Represents a storage backend for [NCachedValue](#cls-NCachedValue) instances.



An `NCachedValueStore` provides persistent or shared storage
for cached values managed by [NCachedValueFactory](#cls-NCachedValueFactory) and [NCachedValue](#cls-NCachedValue).
Implementations may store cache metadata and values in memory,
files, databases, or distributed systems.







This interface abstracts away how and where cached data is stored.
It allows different caching policies (e.g., local memory cache, shared cluster cache,
or disk-based persistence) to be plugged into the caching mechanism.







Example usage:
```java
NCachedValueStore store = ...;

// Load cached model by id
NCachedValueModel model = store.load("user-session");

// Update and persist cache state
model.setValue(newSessionData);
store.save(model);
```




**Since:** 0.8.0

### Method Summary

| Modifier and Type | Method | Description |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `NCachedValueModel` | [load(String)](#m-NCachedValueStore-load) | Loads the cached value model for the given identifier. |

may return `null` or an empty model, depending on the implementation.

<a id="cls-net-thevpc-nuts-concurrent-NCallable"></a>

## NCallable

```java
public interface NCallable<T> extends NRedescribable<NCallable<T>>, Callable<T>
```

A `Callable` extension that integrates with the Nuts element description system.



`NCallable` behaves like a standard `Callable` but adds
support for structured self-description through [NRedescribable](#cls-NRedescribable).
This allows callables to carry semantic metadata that can be serialized,
logged, or analyzed at runtime.







This interface also provides exception-safety utilities for wrapping regular
`Callable` instances so that checked exceptions are rethrown as unchecked
using [NReservedExceptions#ofUncheckedException(Throwable)](#cls-NReservedExceptions).







Example:
```java
NCallable<String> task = NCallable.of(() -> {
    Thread.sleep(100);
    return "Done";);

// Optionally attach a structured description
task = task.redescribe(() -> NElements.ofObject().set("task", "sleep-then-done").build());

String result = task.call(); // No checked exception declaration needed
}
```




**Since:** 0.8.0

### Method Summary

| Modifier and Type | Method | Description |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `static NCallable&lt;T&gt;` | [of(Callable&lt;T&gt;)](#m-NCallable-of) | Wraps a standard Callable into an NCallable. |
| `T` | [call()](#m-NCallable-call) | Executes the computation and returns its result. |
| `default NCallable&lt;T&gt;` | [withDescription(Supplier&lt;NElement&gt;)](#m-NCallable-withDescription) | Associates a new structured description with this callable. |

### Method Details


---

<a id="m-NCallable-of"></a>

#### of

```java
static <T> NCallable<T> of(Callable<T> other)
```

Wraps a standard `Callable` into an `NCallable`.



If `other` is already an instance of `NCallable`, it is returned as-is.
Otherwise, this method creates a wrapper that delegates to `other.call()`.






Checked exceptions thrown by the delegate are automatically rethrown
as unchecked exceptions using [NReservedExceptions#ofUncheckedException(Throwable)](#cls-NReservedExceptions).

**Parameters:**

- `other` - the callable to wrap, may be `null`

**Returns:**

a non-null `NCallable` wrapping the given callable



---

<a id="m-NCallable-call"></a>

#### call

```java
T call()
```

Executes the computation and returns its result.



Unlike the standard `Callable#call()`, this method does not
declare checked exceptions; they are automatically rethrown as unchecked.

**Returns:**

the computed result

**Throws:**

- `RuntimeException` - if the computation throws any exception



---

<a id="m-NCallable-withDescription"></a>

#### withDescription

```java
default NCallable<T> withDescription(Supplier<NElement> description)
```

Associates a new structured description with this callable.



The description is a lazily evaluated [NElement](#cls-NElement) that provides
additional metadata about the callable’s purpose or configuration.







If `description` is `null`, the original callable is returned unchanged.

**Parameters:**

- `description` - supplier of the [NElement](#cls-NElement) description

**Returns:**

a new `NCallable` instance with the given description



---

<a id="cls-net-thevpc-nuts-concurrent-NCircuitBreakerCall"></a>

## NCircuitBreakerCall

```java
public interface NCircuitBreakerCall<T> extends NCallable<T>, NDescribable, AutoCloseable
```

Represents a callable task protected by a circuit breaker.



An `NCircuitBreakerCall` wraps a computation (an [NCallable](#cls-NCallable))
and monitors its execution to prevent repeated failures from overwhelming
the system. It supports configurable thresholds for failures and successes,
and allows custom retry periods.







This interface combines three concerns:


- Callable execution semantics via [NCallable](#cls-NCallable)
- Structured metadata and self-description via [NDescribable](#cls-NDescribable)
- Resilience control via the circuit breaker pattern









The circuit breaker can be in one of three states:


- [Status#CLOSED](#cls-Status) – executions are allowed normally.
- [Status#OPEN](#cls-Status) – executions are blocked due to repeated failures.
- [Status#HALF_OPEN](#cls-Status) – executions are allowed selectively to test recovery.









Example usage:
```java
NCircuitBreakerCall<String> task = NCircuitBreakerCall.of(() -> {
    return fetchDataFromRemoteService();)
.setFailureThreshold(3)
.setSuccessThreshold(2)
.setFailureRetryPeriod(attempt -> NDuration.ofSeconds(5))
.setSuccessRetryPeriod(attempt -> NDuration.ofSeconds(1));

String result = task.callOrElse(() -> "default");
}
```




**Since:** 0.8.7

### Method Summary

| Modifier and Type | Method | Description |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `static NCircuitBreakerCall&lt;T&gt;` | [of(NCallable&lt;T&gt;)](#m-NCircuitBreakerCall-of) | Creates a new circuit-breaker call wrapping the given callable. |
| `static NCircuitBreakerCall&lt;T&gt;` | [of(String, NCallable&lt;T&gt;)](#m-NCircuitBreakerCall-of) | Creates a new circuit-breaker call with a specific identifier. |
| `NCircuitBreakerCall&lt;T&gt;` | [failureThreshold(int)](#m-NCircuitBreakerCall-failureThreshold) | Sets the maximum number of consecutive failures before the circuit breaker opens. |
| `NCircuitBreakerCall&lt;T&gt;` | [successThreshold(int)](#m-NCircuitBreakerCall-successThreshold) | Sets the number of consecutive successful executions required to close a half-open circuit. |
| `NCircuitBreakerCall&lt;T&gt;` | [successRetryPeriod(IntFunction&lt;NDuration&gt;)](#m-NCircuitBreakerCall-successRetryPeriod) | Sets the retry period to apply after a failure. |
| `NCircuitBreakerCall&lt;T&gt;` | [failureRetryPeriod(IntFunction&lt;NDuration&gt;)](#m-NCircuitBreakerCall-failureRetryPeriod) | Sets the retry period to apply after a successful execution in half-open state. |
| `T` | [callOrElse(NCallable&lt;T&gt;)](#m-NCircuitBreakerCall-callOrElse) | Executes the callable, returning the result or delegating to a recovery callable if an error occurs. |
| `T` | [callOrLast()](#m-NCircuitBreakerCall-callOrLast) | Executes the callable, returning the result or the last known successful value if an error occurs. |
| `void` | [close()](#m-NCircuitBreakerCall-close) |  |

<a id="cls-net-thevpc-nuts-concurrent-NCircuitBreakerCallFactory"></a>

## NCircuitBreakerCallFactory

```java
public interface NCircuitBreakerCallFactory
```

Factory interface for creating [NCircuitBreakerCall](#cls-NCircuitBreakerCall) instances.



This factory provides a centralized way to configure and create
circuit-breaker calls within the Nuts concurrency framework. It
manages:





- Bean resolution via [net.thevpc.nuts.reflect.NBeanContainer](#cls-net-thevpc-nuts-reflect-NBeanContainer)
- Persistence or state tracking via [NCircuitBreakerCallStore](#cls-NCircuitBreakerCallStore)
- Creation of new [NCircuitBreakerCall](#cls-NCircuitBreakerCall) instances with or without explicit identifiers






Typical usage involves configuring the factory with a bean container
and store, then using [#of(NCallable)](#m-of) or [NCallable)](#m-of)
to produce resilient callable tasks.




```java
NCircuitBreakerCallFactory factory = ...;
factory = factory.withStore(circuitBreakerStore);

NCircuitBreakerCall<String> task = factory.of("taskId", () -> fetchRemoteData());
```




The factory is designed to support dependency injection, persistence,
and consistent configuration across multiple circuit-breaker calls.




**Since:** 0.8.7

| `NCircuitBreakerCallStore` | [store()](#m-NCircuitBreakerCallFactory-store) | Returns the store used by this factory to persist or track circuit-breaker states. |
| `NCircuitBreakerCallFactory` | [withStore(NCircuitBreakerCallStore)](#m-NCircuitBreakerCallFactory-withStore) | Returns a new factory instance using the given circuit-breaker store. |
| `NCircuitBreakerCall&lt;T&gt;` | [of(NCallable&lt;T&gt;)](#m-NCircuitBreakerCallFactory-of) | Creates a new circuit-breaker call wrapping the given callable. |
| `NCircuitBreakerCall&lt;T&gt;` | [of(String, NCallable&lt;T&gt;)](#m-NCircuitBreakerCallFactory-of) | Creates a new circuit-breaker call with a specific identifier. |

<a id="cls-net-thevpc-nuts-concurrent-NCircuitBreakerCallModel"></a>

## NCircuitBreakerCallModel

```java
public class NCircuitBreakerCallModel implements Cloneable, NCopiable
```

Represents the persisted state of a [NCircuitBreakerCall](#cls-NCircuitBreakerCall).



This model tracks the current status of a circuit-breaker call, its thresholds,
retry periods, last known valid result, and any exception encountered during execution.
It is used internally by [NCircuitBreakerCallStore](#cls-NCircuitBreakerCallStore) and [NCircuitBreakerCallFactory](#cls-NCircuitBreakerCallFactory).







Key responsibilities:


- Track circuit-breaker status ([NCircuitBreakerCall.Status](#cls-NCircuitBreakerCall-Status))
- Maintain failure/success counters to control transitions
- Store retry timing functions for success and failure scenarios
- Hold the last successful result for fallback purposes
- Associate the original caller task ([NCallable](#cls-NCallable)) for execution









Example usage:
```java
NCircuitBreakerCallModel model = new NCircuitBreakerCallModel("taskId")
     .setFailureThreshold(5)
     .setSuccessThreshold(3)
     .setFailureRetryPeriod(attempt -> NDuration.ofSeconds(5))
     .setSuccessRetryPeriod(attempt -> NDuration.ofSeconds(1));

model.setLastValidResult(fetchData());
model.setStatus(NCircuitBreakerCall.Status.CLOSED);
```







The model is [Cloneable](#cls-Cloneable) and [NCopiable](#cls-NCopiable), allowing safe copying
when updating or persisting state.




**Since:** 0.8.7

### Fields

| Modifier and Type | Field | Description |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `private String` | `id` | Unique identifier for this circuit-breaker call. |
| `private Throwable` | `error` | Last exception thrown by the associated NCallable. |
| `private NCircuitBreakerCall.Status` | `status` | Current state of the circuit breaker. |
| `private int` | `failureThreshold` | Maximum consecutive failures before opening the circuit. |
| `private int` | `successThreshold` | Required consecutive successes to close a half-open circuit. |
| `private int` | `failureCount` | Current count of consecutive failures. |
| `private int` | `successCount` | Current count of consecutive successes. |
| `private long` | `openTimestamp` | Timestamp when the circuit was last opened. |
| `private IntFunction&lt;NDuration&gt;` | `successRetryPeriod` | Retry period after successful executions in half-open state. |
| `private IntFunction&lt;NDuration&gt;` | `failureRetryPeriod` | Retry period after failed executions. |
| `private Object` | `lastValidResult` | Last successfully computed result, used for fallback. |
| `private NCallable&lt;?&gt;` | `caller` | Original caller task associated with this model. |

<a id="cls-net-thevpc-nuts-concurrent-NCircuitBreakerCallStore"></a>

## NCircuitBreakerCallStore

```java
public interface NCircuitBreakerCallStore
```

Store interface for persisting and retrieving [NCircuitBreakerCall](#cls-NCircuitBreakerCall) state.



An `NCircuitBreakerCallStore` provides a mechanism to load and save
the state of circuit-breaker calls. This allows circuit breakers to
maintain continuity across application restarts or distributed environments.







Typical usage:
```java
NCircuitBreakerCallStore store = ...;
NCircuitBreakerCallModel model = store.load("taskId");

// Update state
model.setLastExecutionTimestamp(System.currentTimeMillis());
store.save(model);
```







Implementations can persist state in memory, databases, files, or distributed
caches depending on the needs of the application.




**Since:** 0.8.7

### Method Summary

| Modifier and Type | Method | Description |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `NCircuitBreakerCallModel` | [load(String)](#m-NCircuitBreakerCallStore-load) | Loads the state of a circuit-breaker call by its identifier. |
| `void` | [save(NCircuitBreakerCallModel)](#m-NCircuitBreakerCallStore-save) | Saves the state of a circuit-breaker call. |
| `boolean` | [delete(String)](#m-NCircuitBreakerCallStore-delete) | delete its identifier. |

### Method Details


---

<a id="cls-net-thevpc-nuts-concurrent-NCompensationStrategy"></a>

## NCompensationStrategy

```java
public enum NCompensationStrategy implements NEnum
```

Defines how a saga should behave when a compensation step fails.



In a saga, compensation steps are used to undo completed actions when
part of the workflow fails. The `NCompensationStrategy` determines
whether the saga should stop or continue execution when one of these
compensations itself fails.



**Since:** 0.8.7

### Enum Constants

| Constant | Description |
| :--- | :--- |
| `ABORT` | Abort the entire saga when a compensation fails. |
| `IGNORE` | Ignore failed compensations and continue execution. |

### Fields

| Modifier and Type | Field | Description |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `private final String` | `id` | lower-cased identifier for the enum entry |

### Constructors


| `String` | [id()](#m-NCompensationStrategy-id) | Returns the lower-cased identifier for this strategy. |

<a id="cls-net-thevpc-nuts-concurrent-NLock"></a>

## NLock

```java
public interface NLock extends Lock, NDescribable
```

NutsLock extends [Lock](#cls-Lock) with describing capabilities, convenience execution methods,
and support for local, file-based, companion, and distributed locks.

**Since:** 0.5.8

### Method Summary

| Modifier and Type | Method | Description |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `static NLock` | [of(Object)](#m-NLock-of) | Creates a new lock for the given target object, ID, or file. |
| `static NLock` | [of(String)](#m-NLock-of) | Creates a named lock for the given identifier using the default lock factory. |
| `static NLock` | [ofFile(NPath)](#m-NLock-ofFile) | Creates a direct file lock where the specified file IS the lock file. |
| `static NLock` | [ofFile(Path)](#m-NLock-ofFile) | Creates a direct file lock where the specified file IS the lock file. |
| `static NLock` | [ofFile(File)](#m-NLock-ofFile) | Creates a direct file lock where the specified file IS the lock file. |
| `static NLock` | [ofCompanion(NPath)](#m-NLock-ofCompanion) | Creates a companion lock protecting the given file or directory. |
| `static NLock` | [ofCompanion(Path)](#m-NLock-ofCompanion) | Creates a companion lock protecting the given file or directory. |
| `static NLock` | [ofCompanion(File)](#m-NLock-ofCompanion) | Creates a companion lock protecting the given file or directory. |
| `static NLock` | [ofCompanion(NPath, String)](#m-NLock-ofCompanion) | Creates a companion lock protecting the given file or directory using a custom suffix or companion name. |
| `static NLock` | [ofCompanion(Path, String)](#m-NLock-ofCompanion) | Creates a companion lock protecting the given file or directory using a custom suffix or companion name. |
| `static NLock` | [ofCompanion(File, String)](#m-NLock-ofCompanion) | Creates a companion lock protecting the given file or directory using a custom suffix or companion name. |
| `static NLock` | [ofPath(NPath)](#m-NLock-ofPath) | Creates a new path instance. |
| `static NLock` | [ofPathCompanion(NPath)](#m-NLock-ofPathCompanion) | Creates a new path instance. |
| `static NLock` | [ofResource(Object)](#m-NLock-ofResource) | Creates a new instance of resource. |
| `static NLock` | [ofId(NId)](#m-NLock-ofId) | Creates a new instance of id. |
| `static NLock` | [ofIdPath(NId, NStoreScope)](#m-NLock-ofIdPath) | Creates a new instance of id. |
| `static NLock` | [ofIdPath(NId, NStoreScope, String)](#m-NLock-ofIdPath) | Creates a new instance of id. |
| `default String` | [lockId()](#m-NLock-lockId) | Returns the lock identifier, if applicable. |
| `default NDuration` | [leaseDuration()](#m-NLock-leaseDuration) | Returns the configured lease duration, if applicable. |
| `default boolean` | [renew(NDuration)](#m-NLock-renew) | Renews the lock lease duration if supported. |
| `boolean` | [isLocked()](#m-NLock-isLocked) | Checks if this is locked. |
| `boolean` | [isHeldByCurrentThread()](#m-NLock-isHeldByCurrentThread) | Checks if this is held. |
| `default void` | [runWith(Runnable)](#m-NLock-runWith) | Runs the given runnable while holding this lock. |
| `default T` | [callWith(Callable&lt;T&gt;)](#m-NLock-callWith) | Calls the given callable while holding this lock. |
| `default boolean` | [runWithImmediately(Runnable)](#m-NLock-runWithImmediately) | Runs the given runnable immediately if the lock can be acquired without waiting. |
| `default boolean` | [runWith(Runnable, long, TimeUnit)](#m-NLock-runWith) | Runs the given runnable if the lock can be acquired within the specified timeout. |
| `default NOptional&lt;T&gt;` | [callWithImmediately(Callable&lt;T&gt;)](#m-NLock-callWithImmediately) | Calls the given callable immediately if the lock can be acquired without waiting. |
| `default NOptional&lt;T&gt;` | [callWith(Callable&lt;T&gt;, long, TimeUnit)](#m-NLock-callWith) | Calls the given callable if the lock can be acquired within the specified timeout. |

<a id="cls-net-thevpc-nuts-concurrent-NLockFactory"></a>

## NLockFactory

```java
public interface NLockFactory
```

Factory interface for creating [NLock](#cls-NLock) instances.



An `NLockFactory` produces and configures locks backed by a pluggable [NLockStore](#cls-NLockStore)
(e.g. in-memory, file-based, JDBC database, or distributed cache).



**Since:** 0.8.8

### Method Summary

| Modifier and Type | Method | Description |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `static NLockFactory` | [of()](#m-NLockFactory-of) | Returns the currently configured NLockFactory. |
| `static NLockFactory` | [of(NLockStore)](#m-NLockFactory-of) | Creates a new NLockFactory backed by the given NLockStore. |
| `static NLockFactory` | [ofMem()](#m-NLockFactory-ofMem) | Returns a memory-only NLockFactory. |
| `static NLockFactory` | [ofDefault()](#m-NLockFactory-ofDefault) | Returns the default NLockFactory. |
| `static void` | [configure(NLockFactory)](#m-NLockFactory-configure) | Configures the global NLockFactory. |
| `NLockStore` | [store()](#m-NLockFactory-store) | Returns the backing store used by this factory. |
| `NLockFactory` | [withStore(NLockStore)](#m-NLockFactory-withStore) | Returns a new factory instance using the provided store. |
| `NLock` | [of(String)](#m-NLockFactory-of) | Creates a named lock for the given identifier. |
| `NLock` | [of(Object)](#m-NLockFactory-of) | Creates a lock for the given target object (entity, key, or path). |
| `NLock` | [ofFile(NPath)](#m-NLockFactory-ofFile) | Creates a direct file lock where the specified file IS the lock file. |
| `default NLock` | [ofFile(Path)](#m-NLockFactory-ofFile) | Creates a direct file lock where the specified file IS the lock file. |
| `default NLock` | [ofFile(File)](#m-NLockFactory-ofFile) | Creates a direct file lock where the specified file IS the lock file. |
| `NLock` | [ofCompanion(NPath)](#m-NLockFactory-ofCompanion) | Creates a companion lock that protects the given file or directory. |
| `default NLock` | [ofCompanion(Path)](#m-NLockFactory-ofCompanion) | Creates a companion lock that protects the given file or directory. |
| `default NLock` | [ofCompanion(File)](#m-NLockFactory-ofCompanion) | Creates a companion lock that protects the given file or directory. |
| `NLock` | [ofCompanion(NPath, String)](#m-NLockFactory-ofCompanion) | Creates a companion lock that protects the given file or directory using a custom suffix or companion name. |
| `default NLock` | [ofCompanion(Path, String)](#m-NLockFactory-ofCompanion) | Creates a companion lock that protects the given file or directory using a custom suffix or companion name. |
| `default NLock` | [ofCompanion(File, String)](#m-NLockFactory-ofCompanion) | Creates a companion lock that protects the given file or directory using a custom suffix or companion name. |
| `NLockBuilder` | [ofBuilder(String)](#m-NLockFactory-ofBuilder) | Creates a builder initialized with the given lock ID. |
| `NLockBuilder` | [ofBuilder()](#m-NLockFactory-ofBuilder) | Creates a new empty lock builder. |

<a id="cls-net-thevpc-nuts-concurrent-NLockModel"></a>

## NLockModel

```java
public class NLockModel implements Cloneable, NCopiable
```

Represents the state model of an [NLock](#cls-NLock) instance.



This model holds lock identification, current owner, acquisition timestamp,
expiration timestamp for distributed lease management, and re-entrant hold count.



**Since:** 0.8.8

### Fields

| Modifier and Type | Field | Description |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `private String` | `id` | Unique identifier for this lock. |
| `private String` | `owner` | Current owner identifier holding this lock (e.g. |
| `private Instant` | `acquiredAt` | Timestamp when the lock was acquired. |
| `private Instant` | `expiresAt` | Timestamp when the lock lease expires. |
| `private int` | `holdCount` | Re-entrant hold count for this lock. |

### Constructors

```java
public NLockModel()
```

```java

```


<a id="cls-net-thevpc-nuts-concurrent-NLockStore"></a>

## NLockStore

```java
public interface NLockStore
```

Storage and distributed backend interface for [NLock](#cls-NLock) instances.



An `NLockStore` abstracts atomic lock acquisition, lease extension,
release, and state persistence across memory, file systems, databases,
or distributed coordination systems.



**Since:** 0.8.8

### Method Summary

| Modifier and Type | Method | Description |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `NLockModel` | [load(String)](#m-NLockStore-load) | Loads the current lock model for the given identifier. |
| `boolean` | [tryAcquire(String, String, NDuration)](#m-NLockStore-tryAcquire) | Attempts to atomically acquire the lock for the given identifier and owner. |
| `boolean` | [renew(String, String, NDuration)](#m-NLockStore-renew) | Renews the lease duration for an already held lock. |
| `boolean` | [release(String, String)](#m-NLockStore-release) | Releases a previously acquired lock if held by the specified owner. |
| `boolean` | [delete(String)](#m-NLockStore-delete) | Deletes the lock entry identified by id. |

### Method Details


---

<a id="m-NLockStore-load"></a>

#### load

```java
NLockModel load(String id)
```

Loads the current lock model for the given identifier.

**Parameters:**

- `id` - the unique identifier of the lock

**Returns:**

the corresponding [NLockModel](#cls-NLockModel), or `null` if not found



---

<a id="m-NLockStore-tryAcquire"></a>

#### tryAcquire

```java
boolean tryAcquire(String id, String owner, NDuration leaseDuration)
```

Attempts to atomically acquire the lock for the given identifier and owner.

**Parameters:**

- `id` - the unique lock identifier
- `owner` - the owner identifier (e.g. process/thread token)
- `leaseDuration` - the maximum lease duration before lock auto-expires

**Returns:**

`true` if the lock was successfully acquired, `false` otherwise



---

<a id="m-NLockStore-renew"></a>

#### renew

```java
boolean renew(String id, String owner, NDuration leaseDuration)
```

Renews the lease duration for an already held lock.

**Parameters:**

- `id` - the unique lock identifier
- `owner` - the owner identifier that currently holds the lock
- `leaseDuration` - the new lease duration to extend from now

**Returns:**

`true` if the lock lease was extended, `false` otherwise



---

<a id="m-NLockStore-release"></a>

#### release

```java
boolean release(String id, String owner)
```

Releases a previously acquired lock if held by the specified owner.

**Parameters:**

- `id` - the unique lock identifier
- `owner` - the owner identifier

**Returns:**

`true` if the lock was released, `false` otherwise



---

<a id="m-NLockStore-delete"></a>

#### delete

```java
boolean delete(String id)
```

Deletes the lock entry identified by `id`.

**Parameters:**

- `id` - the unique lock identifier

**Returns:**

`true` if deleted

<a id="cls-net-thevpc-nuts-concurrent-NOnceValue"></a>

## NOnceValue

```java
public interface NOnceValue<T> extends Supplier<T>, NDescribable, AutoCloseable
```

Represents a stable, lazily-evaluated value that can be computed once and then reused.



A `NOnceValue` may hold a computed value, indicate whether it has been evaluated,
and track its validity or error state. It extends `Supplier` to provide standard
access to the value and [NDescribable](#cls-NDescribable) for descriptive capabilities.



### Method Summary

| Modifier and Type | Method | Description |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `static NOnceValue&lt;T&gt;` | [ofSupplier(Supplier&lt;T&gt;)](#m-NOnceValue-ofSupplier) | Creates a once value from a supplier. |
| `T` | [get()](#m-NOnceValue-get) | Returns the current value of this once value. |
| `boolean` | [isEvaluated()](#m-NOnceValue-isEvaluated) | Checks whether the value has already been evaluated. |
| `boolean` | [isValid()](#m-NOnceValue-isValid) | Checks whether the current value is valid. |
| `boolean` | [isError()](#m-NOnceValue-isError) | Checks whether the value is in an error state. |
| `boolean` | [trySet(T)](#m-NOnceValue-trySet) | Sets the value if it has not already been set or computed. |
| `boolean` | [trySupply(Supplier&lt;T&gt;)](#m-NOnceValue-trySupply) | Computes the value using the given supplier and sets it if it has not already been set. |
| `T` | [orElseSet(Supplier&lt;T&gt;)](#m-NOnceValue-orElseSet) | Or else set. |
| `T` | [orElse(T)](#m-NOnceValue-orElse) | Or else. |
| `void` | [close()](#m-NOnceValue-close) |  |

### Method Details


---

The supplied value will be computed lazily and cached internally.

a new `NOnceValue` wrapping the given supplier


`true` if the value is valid, `false` otherwise


<a id="cls-net-thevpc-nuts-concurrent-NOnceValueFactory"></a>

## NOnceValueFactory

```java
public interface NOnceValueFactory
```

Factory interface for creating [NOnceValue](#cls-NOnceValue) instances.



A once value represents a lazily computed value that can be cached
and optionally stored in a backing [NOnceValueStore](#cls-NOnceValueStore) for reuse
across threads or executions. This factory provides methods to create
such once values, either with or without a store.



Example usage:
```java
NOnceValueFactory factory = NOnceValueFactory.of();
NOnceValue<Integer> stableInt = factory.of(() -> computeExpensiveValue());
```





**Since:** 0.8.6

### Method Summary

| Modifier and Type | Method | Description |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `static NOnceValueFactory` | [of()](#m-NOnceValueFactory-of) | Creates a new NOnceValueFactory using the default store. |
| `static NOnceValueFactory` | [ofDefault()](#m-NOnceValueFactory-ofDefault) |  |
| `static NOnceValueFactory` | [ofMem()](#m-NOnceValueFactory-ofMem) |  |
| `static void` | [configure(NOnceValueFactory)](#m-NOnceValueFactory-configure) |  |
| `static NOnceValueFactory` | [of(NOnceValueStore)](#m-NOnceValueFactory-of) | Creates a new NOnceValueFactory with a custom NOnceValueStore. |
| `NOnceValueFactory` | [withStore(NOnceValueStore)](#m-NOnceValueFactory-withStore) | Sets the backing NOnceValueStore for this factory. |
| `NOnceValueStore` | [store()](#m-NOnceValueFactory-store) | Returns the backing store used by this factory. |
| `NOnceValue&lt;T&gt;` | [of(Supplier&lt;T&gt;)](#m-NOnceValueFactory-of) | Creates a new NOnceValue with the given supplier. |
| `NOnceValue&lt;T&gt;` | [of(String, Supplier&lt;T&gt;)](#m-NOnceValueFactory-of) | Creates a new NOnceValue with the given identifier and supplier. |

### Method Details


---

<a id="cls-net-thevpc-nuts-concurrent-NOnceValueModel"></a>

## NOnceValueModel

```java
public class NOnceValueModel implements Cloneable, NCopiable
```

Represents a once value that can be cached or computed on demand.



This model holds the actual value, an optional error state, and a supplier
to lazily recompute the value if needed. It supports cloning and copying.



**Since:** 0.8.6

### Fields

| Modifier and Type | Field | Description |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `private String` | `id` | Unique identifier for this once value. |
| `private Object` | `value` | Current value held by this once value. |
| `private Throwable` | `error` | Throwable associated with a failed computation of the value. |
| `private Boolean` | `errorState` | True if the value is in an error state, false if valid, null if unknown. |
| `private Supplier&lt;?&gt;` | `supplier` | Supplier to lazily compute the value when needed. |

<a id="cls-net-thevpc-nuts-concurrent-NRateLimitRule"></a>

## NRateLimitRule

```java
public interface NRateLimitRule
```

Represents a rate limiting rule that controls how frequently actions
can be performed or resources can be consumed within a given time window.



Implementations typically use token-bucket, leaky-bucket, or fixed-window
algorithms to track and limit the number of permitted operations.
Each call to [#tryConsume(int)](#m-tryConsume) attempts to consume one or more tokens,
representing permission to proceed with an operation.



**Since:** 0.8.8

### Method Summary

| Modifier and Type | Method | Description |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `boolean` | [tryConsume(int)](#m-NRateLimitRule-tryConsume) | Try to consume tokens. |
| `long` | [nextAvailableMillis(int)](#m-NRateLimitRule-nextAvailableMillis) | How long to wait (ms) until enough tokens are available. |
| `NRateLimitRuleModel` | [toModel()](#m-NRateLimitRule-toModel) | Serialize current state for persistence |

### Method Details


---


**Parameters:**

- `count` - number of tokens

**Returns:**

true if tokens were available and consumed


**Returns:**

milliseconds to wait

<a id="cls-net-thevpc-nuts-concurrent-NRateLimitRuleModel"></a>

## NRateLimitRuleModel

```java
public class NRateLimitRuleModel implements Serializable, NDescribable, NCopiable, Cloneable
```

Represents the persisted or transferable state of a rate limiting rule.



This model captures both the configuration (capacity, duration, strategy)
and the dynamic runtime state (available tokens, last refill time)
of an [NRateLimitRule](#cls-NRateLimitRule). It enables storage, replication,
or synchronization of rate limiter state across systems.



**Since:** 0.8.8

### Fields

| Modifier and Type | Field | Description |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `private String` | `id` | Unique identifier for this rate limit rule. |
| `private String` | `strategy` | Identifier of the strategy used (e.g., "token-bucket", "leaky-bucket", "fixed-window"). |
| `private int` | `capacity` | Maximum number of tokens that can be accumulated (i.e., the bucket capacity). |
| `private long` | `duration` | Refill duration in milliseconds — defines the rate of token replenishment. |
| `private double` | `available` | Number of currently available tokens at the time of serialization. |
| `private long` | `lastRefill` | Timestamp (in milliseconds since epoch) of the last token refill event. |
| `private byte[]` | `config` | Optional serialized configuration data for custom or strategy-specific settings. |

### Constructors

#### `NRateLimitRuleModel()`

```java
public NRateLimitRuleModel()
```

Constructs an empty rate limit rule model.


#### `NRateLimitRuleModel(String id, String strategy, int capacity, long duration, double available, long lastRefill, byte[] config)`

```java
public NRateLimitRuleModel(String id, String strategy, int capacity, long duration, double available, long lastRefill, byte[] config)
```

Constructs a new rate limit rule model.

**Parameters:**

- `id` - unique rule identifier
- `strategy` - name of the applied rate limiting strategy
- `capacity` - maximum number of tokens that can be stored
- `duration` - refill duration in milliseconds
- `available` - number of currently available tokens
- `lastRefill` - last refill timestamp in milliseconds
- `config` - optional binary configuration data

## NRateLimitStrategy

```java
public interface NRateLimitStrategy
```

A strategy for creating rate limit rules from their persisted models.
Implementations define how a particular rate-limiting algorithm
(for example, token bucket or fixed window) is reconstructed from
its serialized state.

<a id="cls-net-thevpc-nuts-concurrent-NRateLimitValue"></a>

## NRateLimitValue

```java
public interface NRateLimitValue extends NDescribable, AutoCloseable
```

Represents a runtime rate-limited value, capable of controlling access to
a resource or operation based on configured rate limits.



Instances are typically created using [#ofBuilder(String)](#m-ofBuilder), which
delegates to the default in-memory [NRateLimitValueFactory](#cls-NRateLimitValueFactory).



This interface provides both non-blocking (`take`) and blocking
(`claim`) methods for acquiring permits, with optional support
for executing actions or callables once the permits are obtained.





**Since:** 0.8.8

### Method Summary

| Modifier and Type | Method | Description |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `static NRateLimitValueBuilder` | [ofBuilder(String)](#m-NRateLimitValue-ofBuilder) | Creates a builder for a new NRateLimitValue using the default (in-memory) NRateLimitValueFactory. |
| `NRateLimitValueResult` | [take()](#m-NRateLimitValue-take) | Attempts to immediately consume one token. |
| `NRateLimitValueResult` | [take(int)](#m-NRateLimitValue-take) | Attempts to immediately consume the specified number of tokens. |
| `NRateLimitValueResult` | [takeAndRun(Runnable)](#m-NRateLimitValue-takeAndRun) | Attempts to consume one token and, if successful, executes the given runnable. |
| `NRateLimitValueResult` | [takeAndRun(int, Runnable)](#m-NRateLimitValue-takeAndRun) | Attempts to consume the specified number of tokens and, if successful, executes the given runnable. |
| `NRateLimitValueResult` | [takeAndCall(NCallable&lt;T&gt;)](#m-NRateLimitValue-takeAndCall) | Attempts to consume one token and, if successful, calls the given callable. |
| `NRateLimitValueResult` | [takeAndCall(int, NCallable&lt;T&gt;)](#m-NRateLimitValue-takeAndCall) | Attempts to consume the specified number of tokens and, if successful, calls the given callable. |
| `NRateLimitValueResult` | [claim(int)](#m-NRateLimitValue-claim) | Blocks until the specified number of tokens are available, then consumes them. |
| `NRateLimitValueResult` | [claim(int, NDuration)](#m-NRateLimitValue-claim) | Waits up to the given timeout for the specified number of tokens to become available. |
| `NRateLimitValueResult` | [claimAndRun(Runnable)](#m-NRateLimitValue-claimAndRun) | Claims one token and, upon success, runs the given runnable. |
| `NRateLimitValueResult` | [claimAndRun(int, Runnable)](#m-NRateLimitValue-claimAndRun) | Claims the specified number of tokens and, upon success, runs the given runnable. |
| `NRateLimitValueResult` | [claimAndRun(NDuration, Runnable)](#m-NRateLimitValue-claimAndRun) | Claims one token, waiting up to the given timeout, then executes the given runnable if successful. |
| `NRateLimitValueResult` | [claimAndCall(int, NCallable&lt;T&gt;)](#m-NRateLimitValue-claimAndCall) | Claims the specified number of tokens, then executes the given callable if successful. |
| `NRateLimitValueResult` | [claimAndCall(NDuration, NCallable&lt;T&gt;)](#m-NRateLimitValue-claimAndCall) | Claims the 1 token, then executes the given callable if successful. |
| `NRateLimitValueResult` | [claimAndRun(int, NDuration, Runnable)](#m-NRateLimitValue-claimAndRun) | Attempts to claim the specified number of permits from this rate limiter, waiting up to the given timeout if necessary for permits to become available. |
| `NRateLimitValueResult` | [claimAndCall(int, NDuration, NCallable&lt;T&gt;)](#m-NRateLimitValue-claimAndCall) | Claims the specified number of tokens, waiting up to the given timeout, and calls the provided callable if successful. |
| `void` | [close()](#m-NRateLimitValue-close) |  |

<a id="m-NRateLimitValue-claimAndRun"></a>

#### claimAndRun

```java
NRateLimitValueResult claimAndRun(int count, NDuration timeout, Runnable runnable)
```

Attempts to claim the specified number of permits from this rate limiter,
waiting up to the given timeout if necessary for permits to become available.



If enough permits are available immediately, they are consumed and the result
is returned at once. Otherwise, the current thread will block until either
the requested number of permits become available or the timeout expires.



This method is interruptible and may return early if the thread is interrupted.

**Parameters:**

- `count` - the number of permits to claim; must be positive
- `timeout` - the maximum time to wait for permits to become available;
                a non-null, non-negative duration
- `runnable` - an optional action to run if the claim is successful.
                The runnable is executed in the calling thread after permits
                have been acquired. May be `null` if no action is needed.

**Returns:**

a result object describing whether the claim was successful and
any metadata (e.g., remaining permits, wait time)



---

<a id="m-NRateLimitValue-claimAndCall"></a>

#### claimAndCall

```java
<T> NRateLimitValueResult claimAndCall(int count, NDuration timeout, NCallable<T> callable)
```

Claims the specified number of tokens, waiting up to the given timeout,
and calls the provided callable if successful.

**Parameters:**

- `count` - number of tokens to claim
- `timeout` - maximum time to wait for permits
- `callable` - action to execute upon success

**Returns:**

a result describing the operation

```java
void close()
```

<a id="cls-net-thevpc-nuts-concurrent-NRateLimitValueBuilder"></a>

## NRateLimitValueBuilder

```java
public interface NRateLimitValueBuilder
```

Builder interface for constructing [NRateLimitValue](#cls-NRateLimitValue) instances.



Each `NRateLimitValue` may include one or more rate-limit rules
defined via [#withLimit(String)](#m-withLimit) or its overloads.
The builder allows configuring multiple independent limits,
each identified by an optional limit ID.



**Since:** 0.8.8

### Method Summary

| Modifier and Type | Method | Description |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `NRateLimitValueBuilder` | [id(String)](#m-NRateLimitValueBuilder-id) | Sets the unique identifier of the rate-limited value. |
| `NRateLimitValue` | [build()](#m-NRateLimitValueBuilder-build) | Builds a new NRateLimitValue using the configured limits and parameters. |
| `NRateLimitRuleBuilder` | [withLimit(String)](#m-NRateLimitValueBuilder-withLimit) | Begins defining a new rate limit rule associated with the given limit ID. |
| `default NRateLimitRuleBuilder` | [withLimit(String, int)](#m-NRateLimitValueBuilder-withLimit) | Convenience method to define a rate limit with the given capacity. |
| `default NRateLimitRuleBuilder` | [withLimit(String, int, NDuration)](#m-NRateLimitValueBuilder-withLimit) | Convenience method to define a rate limit with capacity and duration. |
| `default NRateLimitRuleBuilder` | [withLimit(int)](#m-NRateLimitValueBuilder-withLimit) | Defines an unnamed rate limit rule with the given capacity. |
| `default NRateLimitRuleBuilder` | [withLimit(int, NDuration)](#m-NRateLimitValueBuilder-withLimit) | Defines an unnamed rate limit rule with capacity and duration. |

<a id="cls-net-thevpc-nuts-concurrent-NRateLimitValueFactory"></a>

## NRateLimitValueFactory

```java
public interface NRateLimitValueFactory
```

Factory interface for creating and configuring [NRateLimitValue](#cls-NRateLimitValue) instances.



The factory provides methods to create builders for new rate-limited values,
configure a persistent store, and define custom rate-limit strategies.



Typical usage:


```java
NRateLimitValue value = NRateLimitValueFactory.of()
     .ofBuilder("myRateLimit")
     .withLimit(10, NDuration.ofSeconds(1))
     .build();
```



**Since:** 0.8.8

### Method Summary

| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `static NRateLimitValueFactory` | [of()](#m-NRateLimitValueFactory-of) | Returns the default in-memory factory instance. |

| `static NRateLimitValueFactory` | [ofDefault()](#m-NRateLimitValueFactory-ofDefault) |  |
| `static void` | [configure(NRateLimitValueFactory)](#m-NRateLimitValueFactory-configure) |  |
| `static NRateLimitValueFactory` | [of(NRateLimitValueStore)](#m-NRateLimitValueFactory-of) | Returns a factory instance that uses the given persistent store. |
| `NRateLimitValueBuilder` | [ofBuilder(String)](#m-NRateLimitValueFactory-ofBuilder) | Creates a new builder for a NRateLimitValue with the given identifier. |
| `NRateLimitValueFactory` | [withStore(NRateLimitValueStore)](#m-NRateLimitValueFactory-withStore) | Sets a persistent store to be used by this factory. |
| `NRateLimitValueStore` | [store()](#m-NRateLimitValueFactory-store) | Returns the currently configured store used for persisting rate-limit values. |
| `NRateLimitValueFactory` | [defineStrategy(String, NRateLimitStrategy)](#m-NRateLimitValueFactory-defineStrategy) | Defines a custom rate-limit strategy with the given name. |

<a id="cls-net-thevpc-nuts-concurrent-NRateLimitValueModel"></a>

## NRateLimitValueModel

```java
public class NRateLimitValueModel implements Serializable, NDescribable, NCopiable, Cloneable
```

Model class representing a rate-limited value.



This class contains the unique identifier of the value, the timestamp of the
last access, and an array of rate-limit rules ([NRateLimitRuleModel](#cls-NRateLimitRuleModel))
that govern its behavior. It is serializable and can be described as a
[NElement](#cls-NElement) for inspection or persistence.



Typically, instances of this class are created and managed by
[NRateLimitValueFactory](#cls-NRateLimitValueFactory) implementations.





**Since:** 0.8.7

| :--- | :--- | :--- |

| `private long` | `lastAccess` | Timestamp of the last access in milliseconds since epoch. |
| `private NRateLimitRuleModel[]` | `rules` | Array of rate-limit rules associated with this value. |


- `lastAccess` - last access timestamp in milliseconds
- `rules` - array of rate-limit rules governing this value

<a id="cls-net-thevpc-nuts-concurrent-NRateLimitValueResult"></a>

## NRateLimitValueResult

```java
public interface NRateLimitValueResult
```

Represents the result of an attempt to acquire or consume permits
from a [NRateLimitValue](#cls-NRateLimitValue).



This interface provides methods to check whether the attempt succeeded,
handle success and failure cases, and optionally execute callbacks
or throw exceptions when the attempt fails.



**Since:** 0.8.7

### Method Summary

| Modifier and Type | Method | Description |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `boolean` | [success()](#m-NRateLimitValueResult-success) | Checks whether the attempt to acquire permits was successful. |
| `NRateLimitValueResult` | [orElseError()](#m-NRateLimitValueResult-orElseError) | Throws a runtime exception if the attempt was not successful. |
| `NRateLimitValueResult` | [orElseThrow(Function&lt;NMsg,RuntimeException&gt;)](#m-NRateLimitValueResult-orElseThrow) | Throws a runtime exception generated by the given function if the attempt failed. |
| `NRateLimitValueResult` | [onSuccess(Runnable)](#m-NRateLimitValueResult-onSuccess) | Executes the given Runnable if the attempt succeeded. |
| `NRateLimitValueResult` | [onSuccessCall(NCallable&lt;T&gt;)](#m-NRateLimitValueResult-onSuccessCall) | Executes the given NCallable if the attempt succeeded. |
| `NRateLimitValueResult` | [orElse(Consumer&lt;NMsg&gt;)](#m-NRateLimitValueResult-orElse) | Executes the given Consumer with a failure message if the attempt was not successful. |
| `NRateLimitValueResult` | [orElseCall(Function&lt;NMsg,T&gt;)](#m-NRateLimitValueResult-orElseCall) | Executes the given function with a failure message and returns its result if the attempt was not successful. |
| `T` | [get()](#m-NRateLimitValueResult-get) | Returns the value associated with this result. |

<a id="cls-net-thevpc-nuts-concurrent-NRetryCall"></a>

## NRetryCall

```java
public interface NRetryCall<T> extends NCallable<T>, NDescribable, AutoCloseable
```

A retryable callable task.



This interface extends [NCallable](#cls-NCallable) and provides a mechanism to automatically retry
failed calls according to configurable retry policies. It also supports asynchronous execution,
recover operations, and custom handlers for processing results or failures.



**Since:** 0.8.7

### Method Summary

| Modifier and Type | Method | Description |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `static NRetryCall&lt;T&gt;` | [of(NCallable&lt;T&gt;)](#m-NRetryCall-of) | Creates a new retryable call for the given callable using the default concurrency manager. |
| `static NRetryCall&lt;T&gt;` | [of(String, NCallable&lt;T&gt;)](#m-NRetryCall-of) | Creates a new retryable call with a specific identifier. |
| `NRetryCall&lt;T&gt;` | [maxRetries(int)](#m-NRetryCall-maxRetries) | Sets the maximum number of retry attempts. |
| `NRetryCall&lt;T&gt;` | [retryPeriod(NRetryPeriodFunction)](#m-NRetryCall-retryPeriod) | Sets a dynamic retry period function based on attempt index. |
| `NRetryCall&lt;T&gt;` | [retryPeriod(NDuration)](#m-NRetryCall-retryPeriod) | Sets a fixed retry period for all attempts. |
| `NRetryCall&lt;T&gt;` | [recover(NCallable&lt;T&gt;)](#m-NRetryCall-recover) | Adds a recovery callable to execute if all retry attempts fail. |
| `NRetryCall&lt;T&gt;` | [handler(NRetryHandler&lt;T&gt;)](#m-NRetryCall-handler) | Sets a custom handler to process the result after completion. |
| `T` | [callOrElse(NCallable&lt;T&gt;)](#m-NRetryCall-callOrElse) | Executes the call, blocking until a result is obtained. |
| `T` | [call()](#m-NRetryCall-call) | Executes the call synchronously, blocking until completion. |
| `void` | [callAsync()](#m-NRetryCall-callAsync) | Executes the call asynchronously. |
| `void` | [close()](#m-NRetryCall-close) | dispose of the retry call and delete it |
| `Future&lt;NRetryResult&lt;T&gt;&gt;` | [callFuture()](#m-NRetryCall-callFuture) | Returns a Future representing the asynchronous execution of this retry call. |

### Method Details


<a id="cls-net-thevpc-nuts-concurrent-NRetryCallModel"></a>

## NRetryCallModel

```java
public class NRetryCallModel implements Cloneable, NCopiable
```

Model class representing the state and configuration of a retryable call.



Used internally by [NRetryCall](#cls-NRetryCall) and [NRetryCallFactory](#cls-NRetryCallFactory) to store the
current status, results, retry policy, and associated handlers or recovery logic.



**Since:** 0.8.7

### Fields

| Modifier and Type | Field | Description |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `private String` | `id` | Unique identifier for this retry call. |
| `private Throwable` | `error` | Last throwable encountered during a failed attempt, if any. |
| `private NRetryCall.Status` | `status` | Current status of the retry call. |
| `private Object` | `result` | Result of the call, if successfully completed. |
| `private int` | `failedAttempts` | Number of failed attempts so far. |
| `private NDuration` | `expiry` | Expiry duration for this retry call. |
| `private NCallable&lt;?&gt;` | `recover` | Recovery callable executed when max attempts are reached without success. |
| `private NCallable&lt;?&gt;` | `caller` | Original callable associated with this retry call. |
| `private NRetryHandler&lt;?&gt;` | `handler` | Optional handler invoked after call completion. |
| `private IntFunction&lt;NDuration&gt;` | `retryPeriod` | Function that calculates the retry period dynamically based on attempt index. |
| `private int` | `maxRetries` | Maximum number of retries allowed. |

## NRetryCallStore

```java
public interface NRetryCallStore
```

Store interface for persisting and retrieving [NRetryCallModel](#cls-NRetryCallModel) instances.



This abstraction allows retry call state to be saved and restored, enabling
persistence across application restarts or distributed systems.



**Since:** 0.8.7

| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `NRetryCallModel` | [load(String)](#m-NRetryCallStore-load) | Loads a retry call model by its identifier. |

| `boolean` | [delete(String)](#m-NRetryCallStore-delete) | delete its identifier. |

<a id="cls-net-thevpc-nuts-concurrent-NSagaCallable"></a>

## NSagaCallable

```java
public interface NSagaCallable<T> extends NCallable<T>, NCopiable, AutoCloseable
```

Represents a saga-style callable that supports stepwise execution, variable storage,
result retrieval, and copy/reset functionality.



A saga is a long-running process or transaction that can be executed in multiple
steps, potentially with compensation or rollback capabilities. This interface extends
[NCallable](#cls-NCallable) to provide a standard call mechanism and [NCopiable](#cls-NCopiable) to
allow creating copies of the saga instance.



### Method Summary

| Modifier and Type | Method | Description |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `T` | [callOrElse(NCallable&lt;T&gt;)](#m-NSagaCallable-callOrElse) | Executes this saga callable and returns its result. |
| `T` | [result()](#m-NSagaCallable-result) | Returns the result produced by this saga callable. |
| `V` | [getVar(String)](#m-NSagaCallable-getVar) | Retrieves a variable stored in the saga context by its key. |
| `NSagaCallable&lt;T&gt;` | [setVar(String, Object)](#m-NSagaCallable-setVar) | Sets a variable in the saga context. |
| `NSagaCallable&lt;T&gt;` | [copy()](#m-NSagaCallable-copy) | Returns a copy of this saga callable. |
| `NSagaCallable&lt;T&gt;` | [reset()](#m-NSagaCallable-reset) | Resets the saga to its initial state, discarding any progress or variables. |
| `NSagaCallable&lt;T&gt;` | [newInstance()](#m-NSagaCallable-newInstance) | Creates a new instance of this saga callable. |
| `NSagaStatus` | [status()](#m-NSagaCallable-status) | Returns the current status of the saga. |
| `boolean` | [runStep()](#m-NSagaCallable-runStep) |  |
| `String` | [id()](#m-NSagaCallable-id) | Returns the unique identifier of this saga. |
| `NSagaModel` | [model()](#m-NSagaCallable-model) | Returns the underlying saga model. |
| `void` | [close()](#m-NSagaCallable-close) |  |

### Method Details


---

<a id="m-NSagaCallable-callOrElse"></a>

#### callOrElse

```java
T callOrElse(NCallable<T> other)
```

is executed as a fallback.

the result of this saga or the fallback callable

<a id="cls-net-thevpc-nuts-concurrent-NSagaCallableBuilder"></a>

## NSagaCallableBuilder

```java
public interface NSagaCallableBuilder
```

Builder interface for constructing [NSagaCallable](#cls-NSagaCallable) instances.



This interface provides a fluent API to define a saga as a sequence of steps,
including conditional branching ([If](#cls-If)) and loops ([While](#cls-While)).
It supports named steps, nested conditions, and nested loops, allowing complex
saga workflows to be built in a readable, declarative style.



### Method Summary

| Modifier and Type | Method | Description |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `static NSagaCallableBuilder` | [of()](#m-NSagaCallableBuilder-of) |  |
| `static NSagaCallableBuilder` | [of(String)](#m-NSagaCallableBuilder-of) |  |
| `NSagaCallableBuilder` | [id(String)](#m-NSagaCallableBuilder-id) | Sets the unique identifier for the saga. |
| `String` | [id()](#m-NSagaCallableBuilder-id) | Returns the unique identifier configured for the saga. |
| `NSagaCallableBuilder` | [store(NSagaStore)](#m-NSagaCallableBuilder-store) | Sets the backing store for the saga. |
| `NSagaStore` | [store()](#m-NSagaCallableBuilder-store) | Returns the backing store configured for the saga. |
| `Suite&lt;NSagaCallableBuilder&gt;` | [start()](#m-NSagaCallableBuilder-start) | Starts the saga definition and returns the root suite for adding steps. |
| `NSagaCallable&lt;T&gt;` | [build()](#m-NSagaCallableBuilder-build) | Builds and returns a fully constructed NSagaCallable instance representing the saga workflow defined via the builder. |

<a id="cls-net-thevpc-nuts-concurrent-NSagaCallableFactory"></a>

## NSagaCallableFactory

```java
public interface NSagaCallableFactory
```

Factory interface for creating [NSagaCallable](#cls-NSagaCallable) instances and their builders.



A saga factory centralizes the creation of saga callables and manages the
[NSagaStore](#cls-NSagaStore) used to persist or retrieve saga state. It provides a
consistent way to obtain new saga builders and configure the underlying store.



### Method Summary

| Modifier and Type | Method | Description |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `static NSagaCallableFactory` | [of()](#m-NSagaCallableFactory-of) | Creates a new default NSagaCallableFactory instance. |
| `static NSagaCallableFactory` | [of(NSagaStore)](#m-NSagaCallableFactory-of) | Creates a new NSagaCallableFactory using the provided store. |
| `static NSagaCallableFactory` | [ofDefault()](#m-NSagaCallableFactory-ofDefault) |  |
| `static NSagaCallableFactory` | [ofMem()](#m-NSagaCallableFactory-ofMem) |  |
| `static void` | [configure(NSagaCallableFactory)](#m-NSagaCallableFactory-configure) |  |
| `NSagaStore` | [store()](#m-NSagaCallableFactory-store) | Returns the NSagaStore associated with this factory. |
| `NSagaCallableFactory` | [withStore(NSagaStore)](#m-NSagaCallableFactory-withStore) | Returns a new factory instance that uses the provided NSagaStore. |
| `NSagaCallableBuilder` | [ofBuilder()](#m-NSagaCallableFactory-ofBuilder) | Returns a new NSagaCallableBuilder instance for defining saga workflows. |
| `NSagaCallableBuilder` | [ofBuilder(String)](#m-NSagaCallableFactory-ofBuilder) | Returns a new NSagaCallableBuilder instance configured with the specified saga ID. |
| `default NSagaModel` | [load(String)](#m-NSagaCallableFactory-load) | Loads a saga model by its unique identifier from the configured store. |
| `default boolean` | [delete(String)](#m-NSagaCallableFactory-delete) | Deletes a saga record by its unique identifier from the configured store. |

<a id="cls-net-thevpc-nuts-concurrent-NSagaCondition"></a>

## NSagaCondition

```java
public interface NSagaCondition
```

Represents a condition used in a saga workflow to determine whether
a particular step, branch, or loop should be executed.



Conditions are evaluated dynamically at runtime against the current
[NSagaContext](#cls-NSagaContext), allowing decisions to be based on saga variables,
status, or external inputs.



Typically used in combination with [NSagaCallableBuilder](#cls-NSagaCallableBuilder) methods
such as `thenIf`, `elseIf`, and `thenWhile`.





**Since:** 0.8.7

### Method Summary

| Modifier and Type | Method | Description |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `boolean` | [test(NSagaContext)](#m-NSagaCondition-test) | Evaluates this condition against the provided saga context. |

### Method Details


---

<a id="m-NSagaCondition-test"></a>

#### test

```java
boolean test(NSagaContext context)
```

Evaluates this condition against the provided saga context.

**Parameters:**

- `context` - the current [NSagaContext](#cls-NSagaContext) containing saga state and variables

**Returns:**

`true` if the condition is met; `false` otherwise



---

<a id="cls-net-thevpc-nuts-concurrent-NSagaContext"></a>

## NSagaContext

```java
public interface NSagaContext
```

Represents the context of a saga execution.



The saga context acts as a key-value store for variables and state information
that are shared across the steps of a saga. It allows saga steps and conditions
to read and modify the execution state dynamically.



Typically used by [NSagaStep](#cls-NSagaStep) implementations, [NSagaCondition](#cls-NSagaCondition)s,
and the saga runtime to track intermediate results, flags, or shared data.





**Since:** 0.8.7

### Method Summary

| Modifier and Type | Method | Description |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `T` | [getVar(String)](#m-NSagaContext-getVar) | Retrieves the value of a variable stored in the saga context. |
| `NSagaContext` | [setVar(String, Object)](#m-NSagaContext-setVar) | Sets a variable in the saga context. |

### Method Details


---

<a id="m-NSagaContext-getVar"></a>

#### getVar

```java
<T> T getVar(String name)
```

Retrieves the value of a variable stored in the saga context.

**Parameters:**

- `name` - the variable name

**Returns:**

the value of the variable, or `null` if not present



---

<a id="m-NSagaContext-setVar"></a>

#### setVar

```java
NSagaContext setVar(String name, Object value)
```

Sets a variable in the saga context.



Variables stored in the context can be accessed by subsequent saga steps
or conditions. Setting a variable with the same name as an existing one
overwrites the previous value.

**Parameters:**

- `name` - the variable name
- `value` - the value to store

**Returns:**

this `NSagaContext` instance for method chaining



---

<a id="cls-net-thevpc-nuts-concurrent-NSagaContextModel"></a>

## NSagaContextModel

```java
public class NSagaContextModel implements Serializable, Cloneable
```

Internal model representing the state of a saga execution.



This class serves as the data container underlying a [NSagaContext](#cls-NSagaContext).
It is not itself a [NSagaContext](#cls-NSagaContext) implementation, but rather stores
all information that the context uses to track a saga's progress, status,
and variables.



The model includes:


- Execution stacks: `stackStepId`, `stackStepGroup`, `stackStepIndex` for tracking nested steps and iteration indices.
- Variable storage: a `values` map to hold key-value pairs shared across steps.
- Compensation tracking: `stepsToCompensate` stack for steps needing rollback in case of failure.
- Status: [NSagaStatus](#cls-NSagaStatus) indicating the current state of the saga (RUNNING, FINISHED, FAILED, COMPENSATING).
- Timing: `startTime` and `endTime` to track execution duration.
- Failure details: the first failed step's ID, name, and exception.
- Last result: stores the output of the most recently executed step.





This class implements `Serializable` for persistence and [Cloneable](#cls-Cloneable)
for creating shallow copies of the saga state, e.g., for branching or retrying steps.







**Since:** 0.8.7
