<a id="cls-net-thevpc-nuts-concurrent-NSagaModel"></a>

## NSagaModel

```java
public class NSagaModel implements Serializable, Cloneable, NCopiable
```

Represents the complete model of a saga execution.



The `NSagaModel` encapsulates:


- The saga identifier (`id`)
- The root saga node tree ([NSagaNodeModel](#cls-NSagaNodeModel)) describing the steps and workflow
- The saga execution context ([NSagaContextModel](#cls-NSagaContextModel)) holding runtime state, variables, and status





This model serves as a snapshot of a saga's definition and current state. It
can be cloned or copied, making it suitable for branching, retrying, or
persisting saga executions.



Implements:


- `Serializable` – for persistence
- [Cloneable](#cls-Cloneable) – for shallow cloning
- [NCopiable](#cls-NCopiable) – for consistent copy semantics









**Since:** 0.8.7

### Fields

| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `private String` | `id` | The unique identifier of this saga execution |
| `private NSagaNodeModel` | `node` | The root node representing the saga's workflow structure |
| `private NSagaContextModel` | `context` | The execution context containing runtime variables and status |

<a id="cls-net-thevpc-nuts-concurrent-NSagaNodeModel"></a>

## NSagaNodeModel

```java
public class NSagaNodeModel implements Serializable, Cloneable, NCopiable
```

Represents a node within a saga workflow model.



Each node can be of type [NSagaNodeType](#cls-NSagaNodeType), such as STEP, IF, or WHILE.
The node contains all information necessary to execute or evaluate it,
including its children, conditional branches, and compensation strategy.



This class serves as the **internal model** of a saga node and is used
by [NSagaModel](#cls-NSagaModel) to define the workflow structure. It is serializable,
cloneable, and supports deep copying via [#clone()](#m-clone) and [#copy()](#m-copy).



Node features include:


- **id**: unique identifier of the node
- **name**: human-readable name of the node
- **type**: type of node ([NSagaNodeType](#cls-NSagaNodeType))
- **stepCall**: for STEP nodes, the actual [NSagaStep](#cls-NSagaStep) to execute
- **stepCondition**: for IF or WHILE nodes, the condition ([NSagaCondition](#cls-NSagaCondition)) to evaluate
- **children**: list of child nodes to execute sequentially
- **elseIfBranches**: list of conditional branches for IF nodes
- **otherwiseBranch**: list of nodes to execute if no IF conditions match
- **status**: execution status of this node ([NSagaNodeStatus](#cls-NSagaNodeStatus))
- **compensationStrategy**: strategy for handling failures ([NCompensationStrategy](#cls-NCompensationStrategy))





Cloning a node recursively clones all children and branches, preserving the
structure of the workflow. This allows independent execution or simulation
of workflow branches.









**Since:** 0.8.7

<a id="cls-net-thevpc-nuts-concurrent-NSagaNodeStatus"></a>

## NSagaNodeStatus

```java
public enum NSagaNodeStatus implements NEnum
```

Represents the execution status of a saga node.



Each node in a saga workflow has a status that indicates its current state
in the execution lifecycle. This includes normal progression, failure, and
compensation handling.



Status values:


- [#PENDING](#m-PENDING) – node has not yet started execution
- [#RUNNING](#m-RUNNING) – node is currently executing
- [#FINISHED](#m-FINISHED) – node has successfully finished execution
- [#FAILED](#m-FAILED) – node execution failed
- [#IGNORED](#m-IGNORED) – node failed but its compensation strategy is IGNORE
- [#COMPENSATING](#m-COMPENSATING) – node is undergoing compensation (rollback)
- [#COMPENSATED](#m-COMPENSATED) – node has been successfully compensated
- [#COMPENSATION_FAILED](#m-COMPENSATION_FAILED) – node compensation failed
- [#COMPENSATION_IGNORED](#m-COMPENSATION_IGNORED) – node compensation was ignored due to strategy





Implements [NEnum](#cls-NEnum) to provide a lower-cased [#id()](#m-id) identifier
suitable for serialization, logging, or display purposes.



Parsing from string is supported via [#parse(String)](#m-parse).









**Since:** 0.8.7

### Enum Constants

| Constant | Description |
| :--- | :--- |
| `PENDING` | ode has not yet started execution |
| `RUNNING` | node is currently executing |
| `FINISHED` | node has successfully finished execution |
| `FAILED` | node execution failed |
| `IGNORED` | node failed but its compensation strategy is IGNORE |
| `COMPENSATING` | node is undergoing compensation (rollback) |
| `COMPENSATED` | node has been successfully compensated |
| `COMPENSATION_FAILED` | node compensation failed |
| `COMPENSATION_IGNORED` | node compensation was ignored due to strategy |

### Fields

| Modifier and Type | Field | Description |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `private final String` | `id` | lower-cased identifier for the enum entry |

<a id="cls-net-thevpc-nuts-concurrent-NSagaNodeType"></a>

## NSagaNodeType

```java
public enum NSagaNodeType implements NEnum
```

Represents the type of a saga node, determining its execution behavior.



Implements [NEnum](#cls-NEnum) for lower-cased IDs and parsing support via [#parse(String)](#m-parse).



**Since:** 0.8.7

### Enum Constants

| Constant | Description |
| :--- | :--- |
| `STEP` | An executable step node that performs a single operation. |
| `IF` | A conditional node that executes its children only if a specified NSagaCondition evaluates to true. |
| `WHILE` | A looping node that repeatedly executes its children while a NSagaCondition evaluates to true. |
| `SUITE` | A container node that groups multiple child nodes sequentially. |
| `UNDO` | A node used for compensating or rolling back previous steps. |

<a id="cls-net-thevpc-nuts-concurrent-NSagaStatus"></a>

## NSagaStatus

```java
public enum NSagaStatus implements NEnum
```

Represents the overall execution status of a saga.



Implements [NEnum](#cls-NEnum) for lower-cased IDs and parsing support via [#parse(String)](#m-parse).



**Since:** 0.8.7

### Enum Constants

| Constant | Description |
| :--- | :--- |
| `PENDING` | The saga has not yet started execution. |
| `RUNNING` | The saga is currently executing its steps. |
| `SUCCESS` | The saga finished all steps normally without errors. |
| `ROLLED_BACK` | Some steps failed, but all required compensations succeeded. |
| `PARTIAL_ROLLBACK` | Some steps failed, and some compensations were ignored or skipped (strategy = IGNORE). |
| `FAILED` | Some step could not be compensated (strategy = ABORT); the saga failed. |

<a id="cls-net-thevpc-nuts-concurrent-NSagaStep"></a>

## NSagaStep

```java
public interface NSagaStep
```

Represents a single step in a saga workflow.



A saga step encapsulates the execution logic for a particular operation
and defines how to compensate (undo) that operation if the saga fails
or a rollback is required.



Implementations should be stateless if possible, as the context carries
execution state and variables.





**Since:** 0.8.7

### Method Summary

| Modifier and Type | Method | Description |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `Object` | [call(NSagaContext)](#m-NSagaStep-call) | Executes the logic of this saga step. |
| `void` | [undo(NSagaContext)](#m-NSagaStep-undo) | Compensates or undoes the effects of this saga step. |
| `static NSagaStep` | [of(Function&lt;NSagaContext,Object&gt;)](#m-NSagaStep-of) | Creates a saga step with no undo action. |
| `static NSagaStep` | [of(Function&lt;NSagaContext,Object&gt;, Consumer&lt;NSagaContext&gt;)](#m-NSagaStep-of) | Creates a saga step with execution and compensation actions. |

### Method Details


---

<a id="m-NSagaStep-call"></a>

#### call

```java
Object call(NSagaContext context)
```

Executes the logic of this saga step.

**Parameters:**

- `context` - the [NSagaContext](#cls-NSagaContext) holding saga variables and state

**Returns:**

the result of the step execution, which may be stored in the saga context



---

<a id="m-NSagaStep-undo"></a>

#### undo

```java
void undo(NSagaContext context)
```

Compensates or undoes the effects of this saga step.



This method is called if a previous step fails and the saga requires
rollback according to the compensation strategy.

**Parameters:**

- `context` - the [NSagaContext](#cls-NSagaContext) holding saga variables and state



---

<a id="m-NSagaStep-of"></a>


Creates a saga step with no undo action.

**Parameters:**

- `call` - the execution function

**Returns:**

<a id="cls-net-thevpc-nuts-concurrent-NSagaStore"></a>

## NSagaStore

```java
public interface NSagaStore
```

Defines a persistence mechanism for saga instances.



Implementations of this interface are responsible for storing and retrieving
the state of a saga, represented by [NSagaModel](#cls-NSagaModel). This allows sagas
to survive application restarts or be shared across nodes in a distributed system.



**Since:** 0.8.7

| `boolean` | [delete(String)](#m-NSagaStore-delete) |  |

<a id="cls-net-thevpc-nuts-concurrent-NScopedStack"></a>

## NScopedStack

```java
public class NScopedStack<T>
```

A thread-scoped stack of values that can be inherited by child threads.



Each thread maintains its own stack of values. The "current value" for the thread
is the value at the top of the stack. Previous values in the stack are preserved
and can be accessed if needed using [#peek(int)](#m-peek) or [#stackSnapshot()](#m-stackSnapshot).



This class is useful for hierarchical or nested contexts where:


- Each thread may temporarily override a context value for a block of code.
- Nested scopes must maintain their own context without losing the outer context.
- The context may depend not only on the current value but also on parent or stacked values.





Values are stored using an [InheritableThreadLocal](#cls-InheritableThreadLocal), so child threads inherit
the parent thread's stack.







### Fields

| Modifier and Type | Field | Description |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `private InheritableThreadLocal&lt;Stack&lt;T&gt;&gt;` | `holder` | The thread-local stack holding values for each thread. |
| `private Supplier&lt;T&gt;` | `defaultSupplier` | Optional supplier to provide a default value when the stack is empty. |

### Constructors

#### `NScopedStack(Supplier<T> defaultSupplier)`

```java
public NScopedStack(Supplier<T> defaultSupplier)
```

Constructs a new `NScopedStack` with a default supplier.

**Parameters:**

- `defaultSupplier` - a supplier providing a default value when the stack is empty


### Method Summary

| Modifier and Type | Method | Description |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `T` | [get()](#m-NScopedStack-get) | Returns the current value at the top of the stack for the current thread. |
| `NScopedStack&lt;T&gt;` | [defaultSupplier(Supplier&lt;T&gt;)](#m-NScopedStack-defaultSupplier) | Sets or replaces the default supplier for this stack. |
| `void` | [runWith(T, Runnable)](#m-NScopedStack-runWith) | Temporarily pushes a value onto the stack for the current thread, executes the provided Runnable, and then restores the previous stack state. |
| `V` | [callWith(T, NCallable&lt;V&gt;)](#m-NScopedStack-callWith) | Temporarily pushes a value onto the stack for the current thread, executes the provided NCallable, and returns its result. |
| `boolean` | [isEmpty()](#m-NScopedStack-isEmpty) | Returns whether the stack for the current thread is empty. |
| `int` | [depth()](#m-NScopedStack-depth) | Returns the depth (number of values) of the stack for the current thread. |
| `List&lt;T&gt;` | [stackSnapshot()](#m-NScopedStack-stackSnapshot) | Returns a snapshot of the current stack values for the current thread. |
| `T` | [peek(int)](#m-NScopedStack-peek) | Returns the value at the specified level from the top of the stack. |
| `boolean` | [equals(Object)](#m-NScopedStack-equals) |  |
| `int` | [hashCode()](#m-NScopedStack-hashCode) |  |
| `String` | [toString()](#m-NScopedStack-toString) |  |

### Method Details


---

<a id="m-NScopedStack-get"></a>

#### get

```java
public T get()
```

Returns the current value at the top of the stack for the current thread.



If the stack is empty, the [#defaultSupplier](#m-defaultSupplier) is used if available.

**Returns:**

the current top value or the default value if the stack is empty, `null` if none



---

<a id="m-NScopedStack-defaultSupplier"></a>

#### defaultSupplier

```java
@NSetter
public NScopedStack<T> defaultSupplier(Supplier<T> defaultSupplier)
```

**Mutator:** Property setter.

Sets or replaces the default supplier for this stack.

**Parameters:**

- `defaultSupplier` - the new default supplier

**Returns:**

this `NScopedStack` instance for method chaining



---

<a id="m-NScopedStack-runWith"></a>

#### runWith

```java
public void runWith(T value, Runnable r)
```

Temporarily pushes a value onto the stack for the current thread, executes
the provided `Runnable`, and then restores the previous stack state.



This ensures that nested or temporary overrides do not affect outer scopes.

**Parameters:**

- `value` - the value to push temporarily onto the stack
- `r` - the runnable to execute; may be `null`



---

<a id="m-NScopedStack-callWith"></a>

#### callWith

```java
public <V> V callWith(T value, NCallable<V> c)
```

Temporarily pushes a value onto the stack for the current thread, executes
the provided [NCallable](#cls-NCallable), and returns its result.



After execution, the previous stack state is restored.

**Parameters:**

- `value` - the value to push temporarily onto the stack
- `c` - the callable to execute; may be `null`

**Returns:**

the result of the callable, or `null` if the callable is `null`



**Accessor:** Property getter.

the number of values in the stack, or `0` if empty or uninitialized



---

#### stackSnapshot

```java
@NGetter

```

**Accessor:** Property getter.

Modifying the returned list does not affect the actual stack.

a list containing the current stack values, empty if the stack is empty or uninitialized

#### peek


Returns the value at the specified level from the top of the stack.



Level 0 corresponds to the top of the stack, level 1 to the next element down, and so on.

**Parameters:**

- `levelFromTop` - the level from the top (0 = top)

**Returns:**

the value at the specified level, or `null` if the level is invalid or stack is empty

<a id="cls-net-thevpc-nuts-concurrent-NScopedValue"></a>

## NScopedValue

```java
public class NScopedValue<T>
```

Represents a thread-scoped value that can be inherited by child threads.



Each thread has at most one value at a time. Temporary overrides are possible
using [Runnable)](#m-runWith) or [NCallable)](#m-callWith).



Note: Only the current value is tracked. Nested or hierarchical contexts are not supported.





### Fields

| Modifier and Type | Field | Description |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `private InheritableThreadLocal&lt;T&gt;` | `holder` | Holds the thread-local value. |
| `private Supplier&lt;T&gt;` | `defaultSupplier` | Supplier to provide a default value if none is set. |

### Constructors

#### `NScopedValue(Supplier<T> defaultSupplier)`

```java
public NScopedValue(Supplier<T> defaultSupplier)
```

Creates a new `NScopedValue` with a default value supplier.

**Parameters:**

- `defaultSupplier` - the supplier used to provide a default value


#### `NScopedValue()`

```java
public NScopedValue()
```

Creates a new `NScopedValue` without a default supplier.



Values must then be provided explicitly or via [#computeIfAbsent(Supplier)](#m-computeIfAbsent).

<a id="m-NScopedValue-computeIfAbsent"></a>

#### computeIfAbsent

```java
public T computeIfAbsent(Supplier<T> supplier)
```

Returns the current value for the current thread, computing and setting it
if absent.



The computation follows this order:


- If a value is already set for this thread, return it.
- If a default supplier exists, use it to obtain a value.
- If still absent, use the provided supplier to compute and store the value.

**Parameters:**

- `supplier` - the supplier used to compute a value if absent

**Returns:**

the current or newly computed value



---

<a id="m-NScopedValue-get"></a>

#### get

```java
public T get()
```

Returns the current value for the current thread.



If no value has been set, the default supplier is used if available.

**Returns:**

the current value, or `null` if none is set and no default exists

<a id="cls-net-thevpc-nuts-concurrent-NScoredCallable"></a>

## NScoredCallable

```java
public interface NScoredCallable<T> extends NScorable
```

Represents a callable value with an associated score.



The score indicates the "validity" or "priority" of the value. A score of zero or
less generally indicates an invalid value. This interface allows creation of
both valid and invalid scored callables, optionally providing a message describing
why a value is empty or invalid.



### Method Summary

| Modifier and Type | Method | Description |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `static NScoredCallable&lt;T&gt;` | [of(int, T)](#m-NScoredCallable-of) | Creates a scored callable with the given score and value. |
| `static NScoredCallable&lt;T&gt;` | [of(int, T, Supplier&lt;NMsg&gt;)](#m-NScoredCallable-of) | Creates a scored callable with a score, value, and an optional empty message supplier. |
| `static NScoredCallable&lt;T&gt;` | [of(int, Supplier&lt;T&gt;)](#m-NScoredCallable-of) | Creates a scored callable from a supplier and a score. |
| `static NScoredCallable&lt;T&gt;` | [of(int, Supplier&lt;T&gt;, Supplier&lt;NMsg&gt;)](#m-NScoredCallable-of) | Creates a scored callable from a supplier, score, and optional empty message supplier. |
| `static NScoredCallable&lt;T&gt;` | [ofValid(T)](#m-NScoredCallable-ofValid) | Creates a valid scored callable using a default score. |
| `static NScoredCallable&lt;T&gt;` | [ofValid(Supplier&lt;T&gt;)](#m-NScoredCallable-ofValid) | Creates a valid scored callable using a default score and a supplier. |
| `static NScoredCallable&lt;T&gt;` | [ofValid(int, T)](#m-NScoredCallable-ofValid) | Creates a valid scored callable with a specific score. |
| `static NScoredCallable&lt;T&gt;` | [ofValid(int, Supplier&lt;T&gt;)](#m-NScoredCallable-ofValid) | Creates a valid scored callable with a specific score and a supplier. |
| `static NScoredCallable&lt;T&gt;` | [ofInvalid(Supplier&lt;NMsg&gt;)](#m-NScoredCallable-ofInvalid) | Creates an invalid scored callable with a supplier for the empty message. |
| `static NScoredCallable&lt;T&gt;` | [ofInvalid(NMsg)](#m-NScoredCallable-ofInvalid) | Creates an invalid scored callable with a fixed message. |
| `T` | [call()](#m-NScoredCallable-call) | Computes or retrieves the value of this callable. |
| `int` | [getScore(NScorableContext)](#m-NScoredCallable-getScore) | Returns the score of this callable in the given context. |

or less generally indicates an invalid runnable. Optionally, an empty message
can describe why the runnable is invalid.

<a id="cls-net-thevpc-nuts-concurrent-NTaskResult"></a>

## NTaskResult

```java
public class NTaskResult<T>
```

Represents the result of a task execution, which can either be a successful result
or an error. Each result is associated with a unique task identifier.

### Fields

| Modifier and Type | Field | Description |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `private final String` | `taskId` | The unique identifier of the task. |
| `private final T` | `result` | The result of the task if it completed successfully; null if there was an error. |
| `private final Throwable` | `error` | The exception thrown during task execution if the task failed; null if successful. |

### Constructors

#### `NTaskResult(String taskId, T result, Throwable error)`

```java
private NTaskResult(String taskId, T result, Throwable error)
```

**Parameters:**

<a id="cls-net-thevpc-nuts-concurrent-NTaskSet"></a>

## NTaskSet

```java
public interface NTaskSet
```

A `NTaskSet` allows you to submit, track, and coordinate multiple asynchronous tasks
in a single logical set. Tasks can be submitted as [Future](#cls-Future), [CompletableFuture](#cls-CompletableFuture),
`Supplier`, `Runnable`, `Callable`, or [NCallable](#cls-NCallable).



This interface supports:


- Waiting for all tasks to complete ([#join()](#m-join))
- Retrieving the first completed task ([#first()](#m-first) or [#firstOnly()](#m-firstOnly))
- Accessing all results or errors individually ([#results()](#m-results), [#errors()](#m-errors))
- Cancelling all tasks ([#cancelAll(boolean)](#m-cancelAll))
- Fluent chaining for convenient submission and execution





By default, tasks use the executor provided via [#executorService(ExecutorService)](#m-executorService)
or [NConcurrent#executorService()](#cls-NConcurrent) if none is set.



Example usage:
```java
NTaskSet tasks = NTaskSet.of()
    .call(() -> doWork(1))
    .call(() -> doWork(2))
    .supply(() -> computeValue())
    .run(() -> cleanup());

// Wait for all tasks and throw if any failed
tasks.requireAll();

// Get all results
List<?> results = tasks.results();
```

| `static NTaskSet` | [of()](#m-NTaskSet-of) | Creates a new empty task set. |

| `NTaskSet` | [add(Future&lt;?&gt;)](#m-NTaskSet-add) | Adds an existing Future to the task set. |

<a id="cls-net-thevpc-nuts-concurrent-NUnsafeRunnable"></a>

## NUnsafeRunnable

```java
public interface NUnsafeRunnable extends NRedescribable<NUnsafeRunnable>
```

A runnable task that can throw checked exceptions and can provide a descriptive element.



This is similar to `Runnable`, but the [#run()](#m-run) method is allowed to
throw any `Exception`. Additionally, it supports producing a structured description
via [NElement](#cls-NElement) and can be redescribed with custom metadata.



**Since:** 0.8.7

### Method Summary

| Modifier and Type | Method | Description |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `static NUnsafeRunnable` | [of(NUnsafeRunnable)](#m-NUnsafeRunnable-of) | Returns the given NUnsafeRunnable instance or null if the input is null. |
| `default NUnsafeRunnable` | [withDescription(Supplier&lt;NElement&gt;)](#m-NUnsafeRunnable-withDescription) | Creates a new NUnsafeRunnable with a custom NElement description. |
| `default NElement` | [describe()](#m-NUnsafeRunnable-describe) | Returns a NElement describing this runnable. |
| `void` | [run()](#m-NUnsafeRunnable-run) | Executes the task. |

### Method Details

<a id="cls-net-thevpc-nuts-concurrent-NWorkBalancer"></a>

## NWorkBalancer

```java
public interface NWorkBalancer<T> extends AutoCloseable
```

Represents a synchronous load-balanced call.
Executes work on multiple workers according to the chosen strategy.



**Design and Usage Notes:**




- This interface provides a flexible mechanism for selecting workers based on
    real-time metrics, worker weights, and custom strategy logic.
- **Worker selection** is fully controlled by the configured [NWorkBalancerStrategy](#cls-NWorkBalancerStrategy).
    Strategies receive all relevant job and worker information via [NWorkBalancerStrategyContext](#cls-NWorkBalancerStrategyContext).
- **Queueing, throttling, and priority handling** are intentionally not part of the load balancer itself.
    These concerns should be handled upstream (before submitting the job) or via strategies
    using the available worker metrics and weights.
- Advanced features like max active jobs per worker or custom retry policies
    can be implemented outside the LB using [NRateLimitValue](#cls-NRateLimitValue) or pre-processing layers.
- The design is intended to be extensible:
        

- New strategies can be registered without modifying the core LB logic.
- Worker-specific configuration allows custom metrics and weights for intelligent scheduling.
- Metrics are exposed via [NWorkBalancerWorkerLoad](#cls-NWorkBalancerWorkerLoad) and [NWorkBalancerHostLoadMetrics](#cls-NWorkBalancerHostLoadMetrics),
    enabling monitoring and observability of active and completed jobs per worker.



**Since:** 0.8.7

### Method Summary

| Modifier and Type | Method | Description |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `List&lt;NWorkBalancerRunningJob&gt;` | [runningJobs()](#m-NWorkBalancer-runningJobs) | Returns a snapshot of all currently running jobs for this call. |
| `boolean` | [isRunning()](#m-NWorkBalancer-isRunning) | Checks whether any jobs are currently active for this call. |
| `int` | [runningJobsCount()](#m-NWorkBalancer-runningJobsCount) | Returns the number of currently active jobs for this call. |
| `List&lt;NWorkBalancerWorker&gt;` | [workers()](#m-NWorkBalancer-workers) | Returns the list of workers currently registered in this balancer. |
| `NOptional&lt;NWorkBalancerWorkerLoad&gt;` | [getWorkerLoad(String)](#m-NWorkBalancer-getWorkerLoad) | Returns the load metrics and counters for a specific worker. |
| `Map&lt;String,NWorkBalancerWorkerLoad&gt;` | [workerLoads()](#m-NWorkBalancer-workerLoads) | Returns the load metrics and counters for all workers. |
| `NCallable&lt;T&gt;` | [of(String, NWorkBalancerJob&lt;T&gt;)](#m-NWorkBalancer-of) | Creates a NCallable wrapping the given NWorkBalancerJob. |
| `NOptional&lt;NElement&gt;` | [getOption(String)](#m-NWorkBalancer-getOption) | Returns the value of a global option configured on this balancer. |

<a id="m-NWorkBalancer-of"></a>

#### of

```java
NCallable<T> of(String name, NWorkBalancerJob<T> job)
```

Creates a [NCallable](#cls-NCallable) wrapping the given [NWorkBalancerJob](#cls-NWorkBalancerJob).



Note that calling this method does **not submit the job** immediately.
The returned [NCallable](#cls-NCallable) can be invoked multiple times; each call:


- Selects a worker at execution time based on the configured strategy and current metrics.
- Tracks execution metrics independently (start/end time, success/failure, active jobs, etc.).
- Notifies the strategy via [NWorkBalancerStrategy#onStartCall(NWorkBalancerStrategyEvent)](#cls-NWorkBalancerStrategy)
    and [NWorkBalancerStrategy#onEndCall(NWorkBalancerStrategyEvent)](#cls-NWorkBalancerStrategy).





This allows for retries, repeated executions, and benchmarking using the same job instance
while maintaining accurate load balancing and metrics tracking.

**Parameters:**

- `name` - a descriptive name for the job
- `job` - the job to be executed

**Returns:**

a [NCallable](#cls-NCallable) that will execute the job when called

<a id="cls-net-thevpc-nuts-concurrent-NWorkBalancerFactory"></a>

## NWorkBalancerFactory

```java
public interface NWorkBalancerFactory
```

Factory for creating and managing [NWorkBalancer](#cls-NWorkBalancer) instances.



This factory provides methods to build new load-balanced calls, manage persistence,
integrate with a bean container, and register custom worker selection strategies.







Users can register custom strategies via
[NWorkBalancerStrategy)](#m-defineStrategy) to extend or replace
the default balancing strategies. This allows defining specialized worker
selection logic beyond [NWorkBalancerDefaultStrategy](#cls-NWorkBalancerDefaultStrategy).







Example usage of a custom strategy:
```java
NWorkBalancerFactory factory = NWorkBalancerFactory.of();
factory.defineStrategy("myCustomStrategy", new NWorkBalancerStrategy() {
    @Override
    public void onStartCall(NWorkBalancerStrategyEvent event) {

    @Override
    public void onEndCall(NWorkBalancerStrategyEvent event) { }

    @Override
    public String selectWorker(NWorkBalancerStrategyContext context) {
        // custom logic to choose a worker
        return context.getWorkers().get(0).getName();
    }
});
NWorkBalancer<String> balancer = factory.ofBuilder("call1")
                                      .setStrategy("myCustomStrategy")
                                      .build();
}</String>
```



**Since:** 0.8.7

| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `static NWorkBalancerFactory` | [of()](#m-NWorkBalancerFactory-of) | Creates a new factory instance with default configuration. |

| `NWorkBalancerBuilder&lt;T&gt;` | [ofBuilder(String)](#m-NWorkBalancerFactory-ofBuilder) | Creates a builder for a new NWorkBalancer with the given unique identifier. |
| `NWorkBalancerFactory` | [withStore(NWorkBalancerStore)](#m-NWorkBalancerFactory-withStore) | Returns a new NWorkBalancerFactory instance configured with the given store. |
| `NWorkBalancerStore` | [store()](#m-NWorkBalancerFactory-store) | Returns the current store used for persisting balancer models. |
| `NWorkBalancerFactory` | [defineStrategy(String, NWorkBalancerStrategy)](#m-NWorkBalancerFactory-defineStrategy) | Returns a new NWorkBalancerFactory with the given NWorkBalancerStrategy registered under the specified name. |

<a id="m-NWorkBalancerFactory-defineStrategy"></a>

#### defineStrategy

```java
NWorkBalancerFactory defineStrategy(String name, NWorkBalancerStrategy strategy)
```

Returns a new [NWorkBalancerFactory](#cls-NWorkBalancerFactory) with the given [NWorkBalancerStrategy](#cls-NWorkBalancerStrategy) registered under the specified name.



Once registered, the strategy can be referenced by name when building an [NWorkBalancer](#cls-NWorkBalancer):
```java
NWorkBalancerFactory factory = NWorkBalancerFactory.of();
NWorkBalancerFactory updatedFactory = factory.defineStrategy("myStrategy", new MyCustomStrategy());
NWorkBalancer<MyResult> balancer = updatedFactory.ofBuilder("callId")
                                                 .setStrategy("myStrategy")
                                                 .build();
```



This allows extending the load balancer with custom worker selection
logic beyond the predefined strategies such as [NWorkBalancerDefaultStrategy#ROUND_ROBIN](#cls-NWorkBalancerDefaultStrategy),
[NWorkBalancerDefaultStrategy#LEAST_LOAD](#cls-NWorkBalancerDefaultStrategy), or [NWorkBalancerDefaultStrategy#POWER_OF_TWO_CHOICES](#cls-NWorkBalancerDefaultStrategy).

**Parameters:**

- `name` - unique name of the strategy
- `strategy` - the custom strategy implementation

**Returns:**

a new [NWorkBalancerFactory](#cls-NWorkBalancerFactory) instance including the registered strategy



---

<a id="cls-net-thevpc-nuts-concurrent-NWorkBalancerHostLoadMetricProvider"></a>

## NWorkBalancerHostLoadMetricProvider

```java
public interface NWorkBalancerHostLoadMetricProvider
```

Provides host load and runtime metrics for a worker.



Implementations of this interface supply the [NWorkBalancerHostLoadMetrics](#cls-NWorkBalancerHostLoadMetrics)
that are used by load balancing strategies to determine the current load,
capacity pressure, and responsiveness of a worker.






The returned metrics may include CPU load, memory usage, latency, or
any custom metrics that influence worker selection in a strategy such as
[NWorkBalancerDefaultStrategy#LEAST_LOAD](#cls-NWorkBalancerDefaultStrategy) or a custom strategy.






A typical implementation collects real-time data from the host system or
from the worker itself. This allows the [NWorkBalancer](#cls-NWorkBalancer) to make
informed decisions when selecting the best worker for executing a job.






Example usage:
```java
NWorkBalancerHostLoadMetricProvider provider = () -> {
    NWorkBalancerHostLoadMetrics metrics = new NWorkBalancerHostLoadMetrics();
    metrics.setHostCpuLoad(getCpuLoad());
    metrics.setResolveHostMemoryLoad(getMemoryLoad());
    metrics.setHostLatency(measureLatency());
    return metrics;;
}
```




**Since:** 0.8.7

| Modifier and Type | Method | Description |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `NWorkBalancerHostLoadMetrics` | [resolveDefaultMetrics()](#m-NWorkBalancerHostLoadMetricProvider-resolveDefaultMetrics) | Resolves the default host load metrics for this worker. |

---

<a id="cls-net-thevpc-nuts-concurrent-NWorkBalancerHostLoadMetrics"></a>

## NWorkBalancerHostLoadMetrics

```java
public class NWorkBalancerHostLoadMetrics implements NCopiable, Cloneable
```

Represents runtime load metrics of a worker host.



These metrics are used by [NWorkBalancer](#cls-NWorkBalancer) strategies to evaluate
the load, responsiveness, and capacity pressure of each worker when
selecting where to execute jobs.






Metrics may include CPU load, memory usage, latency, or any custom
metrics defined by the user. All metrics are optional; strategies
may ignore metrics that are unavailable (NaN or negative values).






Typical usage:
```java
NWorkBalancerHostLoadMetrics metrics = new NWorkBalancerHostLoadMetrics()
    .setHostLoadFactor(0.75f)
    .setHostCpuLoad(0.65f)
    .setHostMemoryLoad(0.55f)
    .setHostLatency(120L);
```




**Since:** 0.8.7

<a id="cls-net-thevpc-nuts-concurrent-NWorkBalancerJob"></a>

## NWorkBalancerJob

```java
public interface NWorkBalancerJob<T>
```

Represents a unit of work that can be executed by a worker
within an [NWorkBalancer](#cls-NWorkBalancer).



Implementations of this interface contain the logic of the job to
perform. Each job receives a [NWorkBalancerJobContext](#cls-NWorkBalancerJobContext) providing
information about the job itself, the worker, and any relevant options.






Jobs should be stateless or handle their own internal state safely,
because the same job instance may be wrapped multiple times as an
[NCallable](#cls-NCallable) using [NWorkBalancerJob)](#cls-NWorkBalancer)
and executed multiple times.




**Since:** 0.8.7

### Method Summary

| Modifier and Type | Method | Description |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `T` | [call(NWorkBalancerJobContext)](#m-NWorkBalancerJob-call) | Executes the job using the provided NWorkBalancerJobContext. |

### Method Details


---

<a id="m-NWorkBalancerJob-call"></a>

#### call

```java
T call(NWorkBalancerJobContext context)
```

Executes the job using the provided [NWorkBalancerJobContext](#cls-NWorkBalancerJobContext).



This method is called by the [NWorkBalancer](#cls-NWorkBalancer) when the job
is executed. The context provides:


- Job identifier and name
- Assigned worker information
- Access to global and worker-specific options








Any exception thrown by this method will be captured by the
[NWorkBalancer](#cls-NWorkBalancer) and reported in the strategy events.

**Parameters:**

- `context` - provides job and worker details, as well as options

**Returns:**

the result of the job execution

**Throws:**

- `RuntimeException` - if the job fails during execution



---

<a id="cls-net-thevpc-nuts-concurrent-NWorkBalancerJobContext"></a>

## NWorkBalancerJobContext

```java
public interface NWorkBalancerJobContext
```

Provides contextual information about a job being executed
within an [NWorkBalancer](#cls-NWorkBalancer).



This context is passed to each [NWorkBalancerJob](#cls-NWorkBalancerJob) when executed,
giving access to job-specific metadata, the assigned worker, and options.






The context allows the job to be aware of:


- Its unique identifier and name
- The worker assigned to execute it
- Any global or worker-specific options






**Since:** 0.8.7

### Method Summary

| Modifier and Type | Method | Description |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `String` | [jobId()](#m-NWorkBalancerJobContext-jobId) | Returns the unique identifier of this job execution. |
| `String` | [jobName()](#m-NWorkBalancerJobContext-jobName) | Returns the human-readable name of the job. |
| `String` | [workerName()](#m-NWorkBalancerJobContext-workerName) | Returns the name of the worker assigned to execute this job. |
| `int` | [workerIndex()](#m-NWorkBalancerJobContext-workerIndex) | Worker index. |
| `int` | [workersCount()](#m-NWorkBalancerJobContext-workersCount) | Workers count. |
| `NWorkBalancerWorker` | [worker()](#m-NWorkBalancerJobContext-worker) | Returns the NWorkBalancerWorker instance assigned to execute this job. |
| `Map&lt;String,NElement&gt;` | [options()](#m-NWorkBalancerJobContext-options) | Returns all options available for this job, including global and worker-specific options. |
| `NOptional&lt;NElement&gt;` | [getOption(String)](#m-NWorkBalancerJobContext-getOption) | Returns the value of a specific option by name, if present. |

<a id="cls-net-thevpc-nuts-concurrent-NWorkBalancerModel"></a>

## NWorkBalancerModel

```java
public class NWorkBalancerModel implements Serializable, Cloneable, NCopiable
```

Model representation of a [NWorkBalancer](#cls-NWorkBalancer).



Holds the ID, strategy, worker configurations, options, and runtime context.
Primarily used as a basis for persistence, storing and restoring
[NWorkBalancer](#cls-NWorkBalancer) configurations.
Can also be used to create or reconstruct [NWorkBalancer](#cls-NWorkBalancer) instances.







The [#copy()](#m-copy) and [#clone()](#m-clone) methods provide safe deep copies,
mainly useful for persistence or snapshotting purposes.




**Since:** 0.8.7

| :--- | :--- | :--- |

| `private String` | `strategy` | Name of the strategy used by this work balancer. |
| `private List&lt;NWorkBalancerWorkerModel&gt;` | `workers` | List of worker configurations participating in this work balancer. |
| `private NWorkBalancerModelContext` | `context` | Contextual metadata for this work balancer. |

<a id="cls-net-thevpc-nuts-concurrent-NWorkBalancerRunningJob"></a>

## NWorkBalancerRunningJob

```java
public interface NWorkBalancerRunningJob
```

Represents a single execution of a job by a worker within an [NWorkBalancer](#cls-NWorkBalancer).



This interface provides access to runtime metadata about a job execution,
including start and end times, duration, executing worker, and any thrown exceptions.
It can be used for monitoring, logging, or metrics aggregation.




**Since:** 0.8.7

### Method Summary

| Modifier and Type | Method | Description |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `String` | [jobId()](#m-NWorkBalancerRunningJob-jobId) | Returns the unique identifier of this job execution. |
| `String` | [jobName()](#m-NWorkBalancerRunningJob-jobName) | Returns the human-readable name of this job. |
| `Instant` | [startTime()](#m-NWorkBalancerRunningJob-startTime) | Returns the wall-clock start time of this job. |
| `long` | [startTimeNano()](#m-NWorkBalancerRunningJob-startTimeNano) | Returns the start time of the job in nanoseconds. |
| `long` | [endTimeNano()](#m-NWorkBalancerRunningJob-endTimeNano) | Returns the end time of the job in nanoseconds. |
| `NDuration` | [duration()](#m-NWorkBalancerRunningJob-duration) | Returns the duration of the job so far. |
| `String` | [workerName()](#m-NWorkBalancerRunningJob-workerName) | Returns the name of the worker executing this job. |
| `boolean` | [isRunning()](#m-NWorkBalancerRunningJob-isRunning) | Returns true if the job is currently running. |
| `Throwable` | [error()](#m-NWorkBalancerRunningJob-error) | Returns any exception thrown during the job execution, if any. |

<a id="cls-net-thevpc-nuts-concurrent-NWorkBalancerStrategy"></a>

## NWorkBalancerStrategy

```java
public interface NWorkBalancerStrategy
```

Strategy interface for selecting a worker in a [NWorkBalancer](#cls-NWorkBalancer).



Implementations of this interface define how jobs are assigned to workers
based on metrics, weights, or custom logic.






Strategies can also listen to job execution events via the [#onStartCall(NWorkBalancerStrategyEvent)](#m-onStartCall)
and [#onEndCall(NWorkBalancerStrategyEvent)](#m-onEndCall) methods. These methods have
default empty implementations, so they can be optionally overridden.




**Since:** 0.8.7

### Method Summary

| Modifier and Type | Method | Description |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `default void` | [onStartCall(NWorkBalancerStrategyEvent)](#m-NWorkBalancerStrategy-onStartCall) | Called when a job starts execution. |
| `default void` | [onEndCall(NWorkBalancerStrategyEvent)](#m-NWorkBalancerStrategy-onEndCall) | Called when a job ends execution. |
| `String` | [selectWorker(NWorkBalancerStrategyContext)](#m-NWorkBalancerStrategy-selectWorker) | Selects a worker from the available workers provided in the context. |

### Method Details


---

<a id="m-NWorkBalancerStrategy-onStartCall"></a>

#### onStartCall

```java
default void onStartCall(NWorkBalancerStrategyEvent event)
```

Called when a job starts execution.



The default implementation does nothing. Override this method to track
metrics, logging, or trigger other side effects when a job starts.

**Parameters:**

- `event` - details about the job and selected worker



---

<a id="m-NWorkBalancerStrategy-onEndCall"></a>

#### onEndCall

```java
default void onEndCall(NWorkBalancerStrategyEvent event)
```

Called when a job ends execution.



The default implementation does nothing. Override this method to track
metrics, logging, or trigger other side effects when a job ends.

**Parameters:**

- `event` - details about the job, selected worker, and any error thrown



---

<a id="m-NWorkBalancerStrategy-selectWorker"></a>

#### selectWorker

```java
String selectWorker(NWorkBalancerStrategyContext context)
```

Selects a worker from the available workers provided in the context.



Implementations should return the **unique name** of the chosen worker
based on the strategy's logic, such as least load, round-robin, or a custom weighting.

**Parameters:**

- `context` - provides access to worker metrics, options, and other runtime data

**Returns:**

the <b>unique name</b> of the selected worker



---

<a id="cls-net-thevpc-nuts-concurrent-NWorkBalancerStrategyContext"></a>

## NWorkBalancerStrategyContext

```java
public interface NWorkBalancerStrategyContext
```

Context provided to [NWorkBalancerStrategy](#cls-NWorkBalancerStrategy) implementations when selecting workers.



This interface gives strategies access to runtime information about workers, their load,
call options, and user-defined variables. It allows strategies to both read metrics
and store temporary state relevant to the current load balancing decision.






Variables can be set globally using [NElement)](#m-setSharedVar) or per worker
using [String, NElement)](#m-setWorkerVar). These variables can influence
future strategy decisions within the same [NWorkBalancer](#cls-NWorkBalancer) instance.




**Since:** 0.8.7

### Method Summary

| Modifier and Type | Method | Description |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `List&lt;NWorkBalancerWorker&gt;` | [workers()](#m-NWorkBalancerStrategyContext-workers) | Returns the list of available workers. |
| `NOptional&lt;NWorkBalancerWorkerLoad&gt;` | [getWorkerLoad(String)](#m-NWorkBalancerStrategyContext-getWorkerLoad) | Returns the current load metrics of a specific worker. |
| `Map&lt;String,NWorkBalancerWorkerLoad&gt;` | [workerLoads()](#m-NWorkBalancerStrategyContext-workerLoads) | Returns the load metrics of all workers in a map keyed by worker name. |
| `NOptional&lt;NElement&gt;` | [getOption(String)](#m-NWorkBalancerStrategyContext-getOption) | Returns a global option for the current call. |
| `Map&lt;String,NElement&gt;` | [options()](#m-NWorkBalancerStrategyContext-options) | Returns all global options for the current call. |
| `NOptional&lt;NElement&gt;` | [getWorkerVar(String, String)](#m-NWorkBalancerStrategyContext-getWorkerVar) | Returns a variable specific to a worker. |
| `NOptional&lt;NElement&gt;` | [getSharedVar(String)](#m-NWorkBalancerStrategyContext-getSharedVar) | Returns a global variable. |
| `NWorkBalancerStrategyContext` | [setWorkerVar(String, String, NElement)](#m-NWorkBalancerStrategyContext-setWorkerVar) | Sets a variable specific to a worker. |
| `NWorkBalancerStrategyContext` | [setSharedVar(String, NElement)](#m-NWorkBalancerStrategyContext-setSharedVar) | Sets a global variable for the current call. |

<a id="cls-net-thevpc-nuts-concurrent-NWorkBalancerStrategyEvent"></a>

## NWorkBalancerStrategyEvent

```java
public class NWorkBalancerStrategyEvent implements java.io.Serializable
```

Event representing the execution lifecycle of a single job in a [NWorkBalancer](#cls-NWorkBalancer).



Instances of this class are passed to [NWorkBalancerStrategy#onStartCall(NWorkBalancerStrategyEvent)](#cls-NWorkBalancerStrategy)
and [NWorkBalancerStrategy#onEndCall(NWorkBalancerStrategyEvent)](#cls-NWorkBalancerStrategy) to allow strategies
to track job progress, update metrics, or implement custom logging/monitoring.






All fields are immutable. The `workerName` may be `null` in the start event
if no worker has been selected yet. The `endTimeNanos` is zero for start events
and set to the actual end time when the job finishes.
The `error` is non-null only if the job threw an exception during execution.




**Since:** 0.8.7

### Fields

| Modifier and Type | Field | Description |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `private final String` | `jobId` | Unique identifier of the job for this call |
| `private final String` | `jobName` | Name of the job |
| `private final String` | `workerName` | Name of the selected worker executing this job, may be null on start |
| `private final long` | `startTimeNanos` | Start time of the job in nanoseconds, set when job starts |
| `private final long` | `endTimeNanos` | End time of the job in nanoseconds, 0 if job has not ended |
| `private final Throwable` | `error` | Non-null if an error occurred during job execution |

### Constructors

#### `NWorkBalancerStrategyEvent(String jobId, String jobName, String workerName, long startTimeNanos, long endTimeNanos, Throwable error)`

```java
public NWorkBalancerStrategyEvent(String jobId, String jobName, String workerName, long startTimeNanos, long endTimeNanos, Throwable error)
```

Constructs a new strategy event.

**Parameters:**

- `jobId` - unique job identifier
- `jobName` - job name
- `workerName` - selected worker name, may be null on start
- `startTimeNanos` - start time in nanoseconds
- `endTimeNanos` - end time in nanoseconds, 0 if not ended
- `error` - the thrown exception, or null if none occurred


### Method Summary

| :--- | :--- | :--- |

| `String` | [jobId()](#m-NWorkBalancerStrategyEvent-jobId) | Returns the job unique ID |

| `long` | [startTimeNanos()](#m-NWorkBalancerStrategyEvent-startTimeNanos) | Returns the job start time in nanoseconds |
| `long` | [endTimeNanos()](#m-NWorkBalancerStrategyEvent-endTimeNanos) | Returns the job end time in nanoseconds, 0 if not finished |
| `Throwable` | [error()](#m-NWorkBalancerStrategyEvent-error) | Returns the error thrown during execution, or null if none |
| `boolean` | [equals(Object)](#m-NWorkBalancerStrategyEvent-equals) |  |
| `int` | [hashCode()](#m-NWorkBalancerStrategyEvent-hashCode) |  |
| `String` | [toString()](#m-NWorkBalancerStrategyEvent-toString) |  |

<a id="cls-net-thevpc-nuts-concurrent-NWorkBalancerWorker"></a>

## NWorkBalancerWorker

```java
public interface NWorkBalancerWorker
```

Represents a worker in a [NWorkBalancer](#cls-NWorkBalancer).



A worker is an execution target for jobs submitted to the work balancer.
Each worker may have a weight, custom options, and a host load metrics provider
that strategies can use to decide which worker to select.




**Since:** 0.8.7

### Method Summary

| Modifier and Type | Method | Description |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `String` | [name()](#m-NWorkBalancerWorker-name) | Returns the unique name of this worker. |
| `NWorkBalancerHostLoadMetricProvider` | [hostLoadMetricsProvider()](#m-NWorkBalancerWorker-hostLoadMetricsProvider) | Returns the host load metrics provider for this worker. |
| `float` | [weight()](#m-NWorkBalancerWorker-weight) | Returns the weight of this worker. |
| `Map&lt;String,NElement&gt;` | [options()](#m-NWorkBalancerWorker-options) | Returns the custom options associated with this worker. |
| `NOptional&lt;NElement&gt;` | [getOption(String)](#m-NWorkBalancerWorker-getOption) | Returns the value of a specific option for this worker. |

### Method Details


---

<a id="m-NWorkBalancerWorker-name"></a>

#### name

```java
@NGetter
String name()
```

**Accessor:** Property getter.

Returns the unique name of this worker.
This name is used by strategies and running jobs to identify the worker.

**Returns:**

the unique worker name



---

#### hostLoadMetricsProvider

```java
@NGetter
NWorkBalancerHostLoadMetricProvider hostLoadMetricsProvider()
```

**Accessor:** Property getter.

Returns the host load metrics provider for this worker.
Strategies may use these metrics to compute worker pressure
and make load-balancing decisions.

**Returns:**

the metrics provider, may be null if not provided



---

<a id="m-NWorkBalancerWorker-weight"></a>

#### weight

```java
@NGetter
float weight()
```

**Accessor:** Property getter.

Returns the weight of this worker.
Weights can be used by strategies such as weighted round-robin
to favor more capable or faster workers.

**Returns:**

the worker weight, default is 1.0



---

<a id="m-NWorkBalancerWorker-options"></a>

#### options

```java
@NGetter
Map<String,NElement> options()
```

**Accessor:** Property getter.

Returns the custom options associated with this worker.
Options can be used by strategies or jobs for custom behavior.

**Returns:**

an unmodifiable map of option names to values



---

<a id="m-NWorkBalancerWorker-getOption"></a>

#### getOption


the option value, or empty if not present

<a id="cls-net-thevpc-nuts-concurrent-NWorkBalancerWorkerLoad"></a>

## NWorkBalancerWorkerLoad

```java
public interface NWorkBalancerWorkerLoad
```

Represents the runtime load and metrics of a [NWorkBalancerWorker](#cls-NWorkBalancerWorker).



All values reflect the state of the worker at a point in time, or are monotonic counters.
Useful for strategies to make informed decisions about worker selection.




**Since:** 0.8.7

### Method Summary

| Modifier and Type | Method | Description |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `NCachedValue&lt;NWorkBalancerHostLoadMetrics&gt;` | [hostLoadMetrics()](#m-NWorkBalancerWorkerLoad-hostLoadMetrics) | Returns cached host load metrics for this worker. |
| `long` | [activeJobsCount()](#m-NWorkBalancerWorkerLoad-activeJobsCount) | Returns the number of currently active jobs on this worker. |
| `long` | [totalJobsCount()](#m-NWorkBalancerWorkerLoad-totalJobsCount) | Returns the total number of job starts on this worker since initialization. |
| `default long` | [completedJobsCount()](#m-NWorkBalancerWorkerLoad-completedJobsCount) | Returns the total number of completed jobs (both succeeded and failed). |
| `long` | [succeededJobCount()](#m-NWorkBalancerWorkerLoad-succeededJobCount) | Returns the total number of succeeded jobs. |
| `long` | [failedJobsCount()](#m-NWorkBalancerWorkerLoad-failedJobsCount) | Returns the total number of failed jobs. |
| `long` | [activeJobsTotalDurationNano()](#m-NWorkBalancerWorkerLoad-activeJobsTotalDurationNano) | Returns the total CPU or wall-clock time (in nanoseconds) currently consumed by all active jobs on this worker. |
| `long` | [completedJobsTotalDurationNano()](#m-NWorkBalancerWorkerLoad-completedJobsTotalDurationNano) | Returns the total CPU or wall-clock time (in nanoseconds) spent by all completed jobs. |

### Method Details


---

<a id="m-NWorkBalancerWorkerLoad-hostLoadMetrics"></a>

#### hostLoadMetrics

```java
NCachedValue<NWorkBalancerHostLoadMetrics> hostLoadMetrics()
```

Returns cached host load metrics for this worker.



Metrics typically include CPU, memory, or other system-level indicators,
normalized between 0.0 and 1.0. May be empty if metrics are unavailable.

**Returns:**

the cached host load metrics, or empty if not available



---

<a id="m-NWorkBalancerWorkerLoad-activeJobsCount"></a>

#### activeJobsCount

```java
long activeJobsCount()
```

Returns the number of currently active jobs on this worker.
This is an atomic snapshot.

**Returns:**

the count of active jobs



---

<a id="m-NWorkBalancerWorkerLoad-totalJobsCount"></a>

#### totalJobsCount

```java
long totalJobsCount()
```

Returns the total number of job starts on this worker since initialization.
This is a monotonic counter.

**Returns:**

total jobs started



---

<a id="m-NWorkBalancerWorkerLoad-completedJobsCount"></a>

#### completedJobsCount

```java
default long completedJobsCount()
```

Returns the total number of completed jobs (both succeeded and failed).

**Returns:**

completed jobs count



---

<a id="m-NWorkBalancerWorkerLoad-succeededJobCount"></a>

#### succeededJobCount

```java
long succeededJobCount()
```

Returns the total number of succeeded jobs.
This is a monotonic counter.

**Returns:**

succeeded jobs count



---

<a id="m-NWorkBalancerWorkerLoad-failedJobsCount"></a>

#### failedJobsCount

```java
long failedJobsCount()
```

Returns the total number of failed jobs.
This is a monotonic counter.

**Returns:**

failed jobs count



---

<a id="m-NWorkBalancerWorkerLoad-activeJobsTotalDurationNano"></a>

#### activeJobsTotalDurationNano

```java
long activeJobsTotalDurationNano()
```

Returns the total CPU or wall-clock time (in nanoseconds) currently consumed
by all active jobs on this worker.



Useful for estimating current load and predicting completion time.

**Returns:**

total active job duration in nanoseconds



---

<a id="m-NWorkBalancerWorkerLoad-completedJobsTotalDurationNano"></a>

#### completedJobsTotalDurationNano

```java
long completedJobsTotalDurationNano()
```

Returns the total CPU or wall-clock time (in nanoseconds) spent by all completed jobs.



Can be used to compute average completed-job latency or throughput.

**Returns:**

total duration of completed jobs in nanoseconds



---

<a id="cls-net-thevpc-nuts-concurrent-NWorkBalancerWorkerModel"></a>

## NWorkBalancerWorkerModel

```java
public class NWorkBalancerWorkerModel implements Serializable, Cloneable, NCopiable
```

Model representation of a [NWorkBalancerWorker](#cls-NWorkBalancerWorker).



Holds the worker's name, weight, host load metrics provider, and optional metadata.
Primarily used as a basis for persistence, for storing and restoring worker configurations.
Can also serve as a blueprint to create [NWorkBalancerWorker](#cls-NWorkBalancerWorker) instances.




**Since:** 0.8.7

### Fields

| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `private String` | `name` | Unique name of the worker |
| `private Map&lt;String,NElement&gt;` | `options` | Optional configuration or metadata for the worker |
| `private float` | `weight` | Relative weight for worker selection in strategies |
| `private NWorkBalancerHostLoadMetricProvider` | `hostLoadMetricsProvider` | Provides host load metrics for this worker |
